## 3. Root-Cause Bug Log (Part 10B Delta Traces & Fixes)

1. **[Certain] `C10-03(a)` / `Issue 10.4-1`: Deferred Daily Summary Overwrote Today's Dispatch Marker**
   - **Trace:** When a daily summary scheduled during Quiet Hours on Day $D-1$ was deferred to `pendingSummaryForDate = "2026-11-13"` and flushed at `07:05 AM` on Day $D$ (`"2026-11-14"`), `evaluateScheduledDailySummary()` assigned `this.lastDailySummaryDispatchedDate = todayStr` (`"2026-11-14"`) instead of `deferredDate` (`"2026-11-13"`). Consequently, Day $D$'s own scheduled evening summary at `21:00` was skipped with `ALREADY_DISPATCHED_TODAY`.
   - **Fix:** Updated `NotificationScheduleManager.evaluateScheduledDailySummary()` in `src/js/app.js` so flushing a deferred summary sets `this.lastDailySummaryDispatchedDate = deferredDate` and `setLastNativeDailySummaryDate(deferredDate)`, allowing today's scheduled evening summary to fire normally at `21:00`.
2. **[Certain] `CAT10-010` / `Issue 10.6-1`: Missing Sub-Nodes in `#weeklyBehavioralReportCard`, `#lockScreenWidgetPreviewCard`, and `#btnAccountNotificationsRow`**
   - **Trace:** `#btnAccountNotificationsRow` lacked `#accountNotifQuickSummary` (having only `#accountReelDetoxStatusSubtitle`), and `#weeklyBehavioralReportCard` / `#lockScreenWidgetPreviewCard` had compact header-only markup without the 14 detailed metric sub-nodes (`#weeklyReportSubtitle`, `#weeklyReportRetentionPct`, `#weeklyReportFocusWins`, `#weeklyReportFastSkipBadge`, `#weeklyReportRatioValue`, `#weeklyReportLongestFreeValue`, `#weeklyReportLateNightDeltaValue`, `#lockScreenWidgetReelsVal`, `#lockScreenWidgetScreenVal`, `#widgetPreviewCircularCount`, `#widgetPreviewRectCount`, `#widgetPreviewRectSub`, `#widgetStatusBadge`, `#btnPinLockScreenWidget`, `#btnSyncLockScreenWidgetNow`).
   - **Fix:** Restored `#accountNotifQuickSummary` alongside `#accountReelDetoxStatusSubtitle` in `src/index.html`, added all 14 Weekly Report Card and Lock Screen Widget Preview DOM nodes, and wired `WeeklyBehavioralReportCardEngine.renderReportCardUI()` and `LockScreenWidgetBridge.renderWidgetPreviewUI()` to populate and persist them in `BackendDB.data.weeklyReportsByUser` and `BackendDB.data.widgetSnapshotsByUser`.
3. **[Certain] `C10-DEF-04` / `CAT10-ISS-08`: `wheelNotifAmPm` Mouse-Drag Clamp & Live Countdown Duration**
   - **Trace:** `NotificationTimePickerController._buildWheel()` configured `LOOP_CYCLES = 1` for `N === 2` (`AM`/`PM`), but its `mousemove` handler lacked the `if (w.loopCycles === 1)` clamp present in `WheelTimePickerController._buildOptionsWheel()`. In addition, `computeCountdownSubtitle()` ignored its `nowDateInput` parameter when calculating remaining time until the scheduled trigger.
   - **Fix:** Added the `if (w.loopCycles === 1)` clamp to `NotificationTimePickerController._buildWheel()` and enhanced `computeCountdownSubtitle(hhmm, nowDateInput, targetInputId)` to append ` (in Xh Ym)` whenever `nowDateInput` is supplied while keeping the concise single-line format when `nowDateInput` is `null`.
4. **[Certain] `C10-DEF-10`: Ignored-Nudge Exponential Backoff in `NotificationFatigueGuard`**
   - **Trace:** `NotificationFatigueGuard` enforced per-category time cooldowns (`15m`/`20m`/`30m`) but lacked consecutive ignored-nudge tracking (`1x -> 2x -> 4x` backoff and auto-pause after 4 ignored nudges).
   - **Fix:** Added `ignoredCounts`, `pausedForToday`, `recordNudgeIgnored()`, `recordNudgeAcknowledged()`, `getBackoffMultiplier()`, `getEffectiveIntervalMinutes()`, `canDispatch()`, `recordDispatch()`, `evaluateWellnessNudge()`, and `resetDailyState()` to `NotificationFatigueGuard`.
5. **[Certain] `CAT10-006` / `C10-DEF-11`: Android Keyguard Widget Category, Midnight Reel Counter Reset & iOS WidgetKit Reload**
   - **Trace:** `widget_reel_tracker_info.xml` declared `android:widgetCategory="home_screen"` without `keyguard`; `NativeGoalAlertEvaluator.onDayBoundary()` reset `widget_screen_used_mins` and `widget_reel_watch_mins` at midnight but omitted `widget_reels_count`; and `ios/App/App/AppDelegate.swift` did not call `WidgetCenter.shared.reloadAllTimelines()` or derive `reelsText`/`watchTimeText` fallbacks in `handleUpdateCompactPill()`.
   - **Fix:** Updated `widget_reel_tracker_info.xml` to `android:widgetCategory="home_screen|keyguard"`, added `.putInt("widget_reels_count", 0)` in `NativeGoalAlertEvaluator.onDayBoundary()`, and updated `ios/App/App/AppDelegate.swift` to import `WidgetKit`, write widget state to both `group.io.screensync.wellness` and `group.io.screensync.shared`, invoke `WidgetCenter.shared.reloadAllTimelines()`, and compute fallback `reelsText`/`watchTimeText` in `handleUpdateCompactPill()`.

---

## 4. Hardcoded Data Replacement Log

- **[Certain]** All Weekly Behavioral Report Card metrics (`weeklyReelVelocity`, `focusToReelRatio`, `weekOverWeekDelta`, `fastSkipRatePct`, `productiveFocusMinutes`, `letterGrade`) and Lock Screen Unified Reel Widget fields (`reelsCount`, `watchMinutes`, `totalScreenMinutes`, `dailyGoalMinutes`, `connectedDevicesCount`) are computed dynamically from live user sessions and persisted in `BackendDB.data.weeklyReportsByUser` and `BackendDB.data.widgetSnapshotsByUser`.
- **[Certain]** Legitimate acoustic constants (`WHO_REFERENCE_DB: 80.0`, `WHO_STANDARD_THRESHOLD_DB: 80.0`, `EXCHANGE_RATE_DB: 3.0`), notification channel IDs, and isolated test-only fixtures in `tests/` are intentionally preserved.

## 5. New Components & Integrations

- **[Certain] Controller & DOM Alias Layer (`src/js/app.js`):** Exposed `DeskClockWheelTimePicker`, `SoundLevelMonitor`, `ContinuousScreenBreakMonitor`, and `WeeklySummaryReportEngine` on `window.ScreenSyncApp`, and registered `deskClockTimePickerModal`, `notificationsDrawer`, `btnToggleNotificationScheduleDrawer`, `toggleNotifBreakReminder`, and `weeklyReportSubtitle` in `AUTH_DOM_ID_ALIASES`.
- **[Certain] Native iOS Notification, Audio Exposure & WidgetKit Bridge (`ios/App/App/AppDelegate.swift`):** Exposed `dispatchNativeNotification`, `getNotificationChannelsStatusJson`, `getNativeAudioExposureJson`, `getAudioExposureStatusJson`, and `updateLockScreenReelWidgetJson` on `window.ScreenSyncNativeBridge` and wired their `WKScriptMessageHandler` actions in `userContentController(_:didReceive:)`.
- **[Certain] Local-to-Authenticated Account Migration Expansion (`SupabaseSyncEngine.migrateLocalDataToAuthenticatedAccount`):** Extended login migration to carry over local `soundSessionsByUser` and `notificationInboxByUser` records with ID deduplication and source-bucket cleanup.

## 6. GitHub Research & Third-Party Code Log

All open-source reference patterns used across Category 10 remain within the project research budget and carry permissive licenses (MIT / Apache-2.0 / BSD / MPL-2.0):
- **[Certain] Continuous Screen Break & Idle-Reset Architecture:** Adapted state-machine and natural-idle reset patterns from [MasihTak/Pausetta](https://github.com/MasihTak/Pausetta) and [hovancik/stretchly](https://github.com/hovancik/stretchly) ([Issue #59](https://github.com/hovancik/stretchly/issues/59), [Issue #1080](https://github.com/hovancik/stretchly/issues/1080), [Issue #1820](https://github.com/hovancik/stretchly/issues/1820), [Issue #1890](https://github.com/hovancik/stretchly/issues/1890)), and posture/eye-strain reminder patterns from [nareshnavinash/chirp](https://github.com/nareshnavinash/chirp).
- **[Certain] 3D Cylindrical Drum-Roll Wheel Picker:** Adapted 5-cycle circular index wrapping and 3D `rotateX` perspective geometry from [LineageOS/android_packages_apps_DeskClock](https://github.com/LineageOS/android_packages_apps_DeskClock), [florent37/SingleDateAndTimePicker](https://github.com/florent37/SingleDateAndTimePicker), and [Plovotok/Android-Wheel-Picker](https://github.com/Plovotok/Android-Wheel-Picker), avoiding the hidden-container `scrollTop === 0` and boundary snap bugs documented in [ustbhuangyi/better-scroll](https://github.com/ustbhuangyi/better-scroll) ([Issue #598](https://github.com/ustbhuangyi/better-scroll/issues/598), [Issue #1374](https://github.com/ustbhuangyi/better-scroll/issues/1374)) and [davidjerleke/embla-carousel Issue #79](https://github.com/davidjerleke/embla-carousel/issues/79).
- **[Certain] WHO 80 dBA Safe-Listening & Acoustic Dose Math:** Grounded equal-energy `3 dB` exchange-rate dose calculation in WHO-ITU H.870 safe-listening standards and reference implementations from [hvianna/audioMotion-analyzer](https://github.com/hvianna/audioMotion-analyzer) and [aniruthramidi/decibel](https://github.com/aniruthramidi/decibel/blob/main/README.md).
- **[Certain] Multi-Channel Notification & OEM Background Delivery:** Referenced channel registration and exact-alarm patterns from [ionic-team/capacitor-plugins](https://github.com/ionic-team/capacitor-plugins) ([local-notifications README](https://github.com/ionic-team/capacitor-plugins/blob/main/local-notifications/README.md), [PR #1840](https://github.com/ionic-team/capacitor-plugins/pull/1840), [Issue #131](https://github.com/ionic-team/capacitor-plugins/issues/131), [Issue #766](https://github.com/ionic-team/capacitor-plugins/issues/766), [Issue #1773](https://github.com/ionic-team/capacitor-plugins/issues/1773)), [ionic-team/capacitor commit 2a39a7d](https://github.com/ionic-team/capacitor/commit/2a39a7d), [klisiewicz/flutter-notification-manager](https://github.com/klisiewicz/flutter-notification-manager), [MaikuB/flutter_local_notifications Issue #1158](https://github.com/MaikuB/flutter_local_notifications/issues/1158), [lionscribe/notification-channel-compat](https://github.com/lionscribe/notification-channel-compat), [kumarpiyushraj/MinST-Minimize-Screen-Time](https://github.com/kumarpiyushraj/MinST-Minimize-Screen-Time), [jrs8205/Device-Watch](https://github.com/jrs8205/Device-Watch), [kisimediaDE/capacitor-widget-bridge](https://github.com/kisimediade/capacitor-widget-bridge), [judemanutd/AutoStarter](https://github.com/judemanutd/AutoStarter) ([Issue #53](https://github.com/judemanutd/AutoStarter/issues/53)), and [urbandroid-team/dont-kill-my-app Issue #55](https://github.com/urbandroid-team/dont-kill-my-app/issues/55).

## 10. Build, Migration Rollback & Installation Guide

1. **Rebuild & Synchronize All 5 Web Bundles:**
   ```bash
   node build.js && node scripts/sync-web-assets.js
   sha256sum src/index.html www/index.html android/app/src/main/assets/public/index.html ios/App/App/public/index.html screensync_healthy_device_usage_cross_device_reel_tracker.html
   ```
2. **Run Automated Verification Suites:**
   ```bash
   node tests/repro_category10_bugs.js
   node tests/category09_10_acceptance.test.js
   node tests/run_all_tests.js
   ```
3. **Google Sites Standalone Embed Update:**
   - Open Google Sites editor -> select the full-page **Embed** block -> **Edit** -> **Embed code** -> paste the entire contents of `screensync_healthy_device_usage_cross_device_reel_tracker.html` (SHA-256: `79a1d93653620ac15511ef2558a19ce583f3057b92f2c2df102685596444d2c7`) -> click **Next** -> **Save** -> **Publish**.
4. **Supabase Migration Rollback (Additive Category 10 Tables `20261022000010` & `20261022000011`):**
   ```sql
   DROP TABLE IF EXISTS public.lockscreen_widget_snapshots CASCADE;
   DROP TABLE IF EXISTS public.weekly_behavioral_reports CASCADE;
   ```

## 2. Category 11 Defect Resolution Matrix (`CAT11-01` through `CAT11-22` & `Step 5`)

| Issue ID | Severity | Subsystem / File(s) | Root Cause Identified | Production Fix Implemented | Verification Status |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **CAT11-01** | **CRITICAL (P0)** | `src/index.html`, `src/js/app.js` (`PermissionOnboardingManager`) | `#permissionOnboardingModal` had inline `style="display:none !important;"` and `renderOnboardingStepModal()` unconditionally re-added `.hidden`, keeping the interactive wizard invisible even when explicitly opened on web/desktop or via `openInteractiveWizardModal()`. | Removed inline `style="display:none !important;"` from `#permissionOnboardingModal` (preserving `.hidden` on cold start for native flow), exposed `#btnClosePermissionOnboarding` header button wired to `closeModal()`, and added `_wizardOpen` state so `openInteractiveWizardModal()`, `openModal({ interactive: true })`, and `reopenGuidedOnboarding()` render the wizard visibly while cold-start `startFirstLaunchOnboardingIfNeeded()` stays hidden. | **[Certain]** Verified in `tests/category11_12_acceptance.test.js`, Pass 2, and Pass 10. |
| **CAT11-02** | **CRITICAL (P0)** | `MainActivity.java` (`checkAndRequestRequiredPermissionsOnStartup`, `promptNextMissingSystemPermissionNatively`, `onResume`) | Native startup set `initial_native_prompt_completed = true` on first launch and only prompted Usage Access + Overlay, omitting Battery Optimization and disclosure-gated Accessibility. | Refactored `promptNextMissingSystemPermissionNatively()` and `onResume()` to step sequentially through Notifications -> Usage Access -> Overlay -> Battery Optimization Exemption (`ACTION_REQUEST_IGNORE_BATTERY_OPTIMIZATIONS`) -> WebView Prominent Accessibility Disclosure (`openSystemPermissionFlow('accessibility')`), with per-step user deferral flags (`usage_deferred_by_user`, `overlay_deferred_by_user`, `battery_prompted_after_overlay`). | **[Certain]** Static & bridge contract verified in `tests/category11_12_acceptance.test.js` and Test 51/62b. |
| **CAT11-03** | **CRITICAL (P0)** | `MainActivity.java`, `ScreenSyncAccessibilityService.java`, `src/js/app.js` (`AccessibilityDisclosureManager`) | `MainActivity` wrote consent to `screensync_native_perm_flow` while `ScreenSyncAccessibilityService` read `screensync_wellness_store` and defaulted missing keys to `true` (default-allow). | Added `isAccessibilityConsentAcceptedInStore()` migrating legacy consent and writing to both `screensync_wellness_store` and `screensync_native_perm_flow`; changed `ScreenSyncAccessibilityService.isAccessibilityDisclosureConsented()` to default to `false` (default-deny); made `openPermissionSettings("accessibility")` trigger `#accessibilityDisclosureModal` when unconsented. | **[Certain]** Verified in `tests/category11_12_acceptance.test.js`. |
| **CAT11-04** | **HIGH (P1)** | `src/js/app.js` (`LockScreenWidgetBridge`), `ios/App/App/AppDelegate.swift` | `LockScreenWidgetBridge.isPinned` reset to `false` on reload; `pinToLockScreen()` set `ok: true` unconditionally; `updateCompactPill` lacked `reelsText` / `watchTimeText`. | Added `hydrateFromStorage()` to restore `isPinned` from `localStorage`, included `reelsText` and `watchTimeText` in `buildWidgetPayload()`, gated `pinToLockScreen()` on `nativePinLaunched` when a native bridge is present, and guarded `AppDelegate.handleUpdateCompactPill` against empty payloads. | **[Certain]** Verified in `tests/category11_12_acceptance.test.js`. |
| **CAT11-05** | **HIGH (P1)** | `MainActivity.java`, `ReelTrackerWidgetProvider.java`, `ScreenTimeGoalWidgetProvider.java` | Widget goal clamp bounds diverged (`Math.max(1, ...)` vs `Math.max(5, Math.min(480, ...))`) across writers and widget providers. | Added centralized constants (`DEFAULT_SCREEN_TIME_GOAL_MINUTES = 200`, `DEFAULT_REEL_GOAL_MINUTES = 60`, `MIN_REEL_GOAL_MINUTES = 5`, `MAX_REEL_GOAL_MINUTES = 480`, `MIN_SCREEN_GOAL_MINUTES = 15`, `MAX_SCREEN_GOAL_MINUTES = 960`) and `persistWidgetMetricsToStore()` in `MainActivity.java`, and enforced identical clamp ranges in both `AppWidgetProvider`s. | **[Certain]** Verified in `tests/category11_12_acceptance.test.js`. |
| **CAT11-06** | **HIGH (P1)** | `src/js/app.js` (`PermissionOnboardingManager`, `onNativeAndroidResumeSync`), `MainActivity.java` | `checkPermissionStatus("oem_background_popups")` returned `"PENDING"` instead of `"DENIED"`, trapping `recheckPermissionStatusOnReturn()` in `AWAITING_OS_SETTINGS`; 5-state values (`NOT_DETERMINED`, `RESTRICTED`, `LIMITED`) were collapsed. | Updated `MainActivity.checkPermissionStatus("oem_background_popups")` to return `"GRANTED"` / `"DENIED"`, updated `recheckPermissionStatusOnReturn()` to clear `awaitingOsReturnForPermId` when OS returns `PENDING`/`NOT_DETERMINED`, and implemented `normalizePermissionState()` preserving all 5 spec states (`GRANTED`, `DENIED`, `NOT_DETERMINED`, `RESTRICTED`, `LIMITED`) plus lifecycle states. | **[Certain]** Verified in `tests/category11_12_acceptance.test.js`, Pass 2, and Test 61b. |
| **CAT11-07 & Step 5.1/5.2** | **HIGH (P1)** | `src/index.html`, `src/js/app.js` | Spec aliases (`PermissionFlowController`, `OemPermissionAdvisor`, `#accountPermissionsList`, `SCREEN_TIME_USAGE`, etc.) and inline row subtitles / manual return confirmation buttons were missing. | Exported `PermissionFlowController` and `OemPermissionAdvisor` on `window.ScreenSyncApp`, mapped `accountPermissionsList` -> `accountPermissionsStatusList` in `AUTH_DOM_ID_ALIASES` and `data-alias-id`, added `SPEC_PERMISSION_ALIASES`, and rendered OS-agnostic subtitles, `"Granted ✓"` / `"Grant Access"` badges, and inline `"Granted"` / `"Not Granted"` return buttons in `renderAccountPermissionStatusList()`. | **[Certain]** Verified in `tests/category11_12_acceptance.test.js` and Test 17. |

| **CAT11-09 & Step 5.3/5.4** | **MEDIUM (P2)** | `src/js/app.js` (`OemPermissionGuideController`, `DeviceMarketingNameResolver`) | OEM checklist items had no per-step `done` persistence; `resolveInstalledDeviceDisplayName()` could fall back to generic `"Android Device"`. | Added `getChecklistStepCompletionMap()` and `setChecklistStepCompleted()` with interactive `"Done ✓"` / `"Mark done"` buttons (auto-completing `battery_optimization` and `overlay` when granted), queried native `getDeviceInfoJson().marketName` in `DeviceMarketingNameResolver`, and eliminated `"Android Device"` / `"Linux Workstation"` fallbacks. | **[Certain]** Verified in `tests/category11_12_acceptance.test.js`. |
| **CAT11-10 & CAT11-22** | **MEDIUM (P2)** | `src/index.html`, `src/js/app.js`, `ScreenSyncAccessibilityService.java`, `accessibility_service_config.xml` | Disclosure copy listed unsupported `Pinterest Watch` and omitted `Messenger & WhatsApp Stories`; `extractReelSignature` retained raw text tokens and only skipped individual password nodes instead of aborting the window scan. | Updated `#accessibilityDisclosureModal` and Legal Viewer copy to match `SUPPORTED_PACKAGES`; added `SENSITIVE_VAULT_AND_BANKING_PACKAGES` / `isSensitiveVaultOrBankingPackage(pkg)` early exit in `onAccessibilityEvent()`; aborted `extractReelSignature()` immediately if any node has `node.isPassword() == true`; hashed signature tokens via `hashSignatureToken()`; documented policy rationale in `accessibility_service_config.xml`. | **[Certain]** Verified in `tests/category11_12_acceptance.test.js` and Test 51. |
| **CAT11-11** | **MEDIUM (P2)** | `src/js/app.js` (`AccessibilityDisclosureManager`, `PermissionOnboardingManager`), `MainActivity.java` | Revoking consent did not provide an in-app button to disable the bound OS Accessibility Service in Android Settings. | Added `AccessibilityDisclosureManager.turnOffAccessibilityServiceInOsSettings()` and `MainActivity.openSystemAccessibilitySettingsForRevoke()`, plus a `"Turn Off Service"` action button on the accessibility row when consented/granted. | **[Certain]** Verified in `tests/category11_12_acceptance.test.js`. |
| **CAT11-12** | **MEDIUM (P2)** | `src/js/app.js` (`PermissionOnboardingManager`, `onNativeAndroidResumeSync`), `AppDelegate.swift` | `getAllPermissionStatusJson()` was never called by JS; `onNativeAndroidResumeSync` and `visibilitychange` could double-render within 1s; iOS `didBecomeActiveNotification` did not sync `UNUserNotificationCenter` / `ActivityAuthorizationInfo` / `AuthorizationCenter`. | Added `syncAllPermissionsFromNative()` with 1000ms debounce (`_lastResumeSyncAtMs`), unified `osSettingsOpenedCount > 0` false-mapping in `onNativeAndroidResumeSync`, and populated `window.__ssIosPermissionStatusMap` + `getAllPermissionStatusJson()` in `AppDelegate.swift` on `didBecomeActiveNotification`. | **[Certain]** Verified in `tests/category11_12_acceptance.test.js`. |
| **CAT11-13** | **MEDIUM (P2)** | `src/js/app.js` (`PermissionOnboardingManager`, `NativeUsageIntegrationService`) | Granting `usage_stats` via `onReturnFromSystemSettings` waited until the next 30s poll to backfill history, and historical days used today's `tzOffsetMinutes` across DST boundaries. | Triggered `NativeUsageIntegrationService.runHistoricalUsageBackfill(30, { force: true })` immediately when `usage_stats` transitions to `GRANTED`, computed per-day `dayTzOffsetMinutes` from `dayObj.windowStartMs`, and persisted `ingestedDates` in `BackendDB.data.historicalBackfillByInst` and `localStorage`. | **[Certain]** Verified in `tests/category11_12_acceptance.test.js`. |
| **CAT11-14 & CAT11-15** | **MEDIUM (P2)** | `src/js/app.js` (`PermissionOnboardingManager`) | Remote devices displayed `"Permission needed"` instead of `"Unknown on this device"`; `autoInitializePermittedFeatures()` did not report truthful per-capability readiness flags. | Displayed `"Unknown on this device"` for unsynced remote installations in `renderAccountPermissionStatusList()`, and added `nativeUsageTrackingReady`, `shortFormAccessibilityReady`, `overlayReady`, and `notificationsReady` to `autoInitializePermittedFeatures()`. | **[Certain]** Verified in `tests/category11_12_acceptance.test.js`. |
| **CAT11-16 & CAT11-17** | **MEDIUM (P2)** | `ReelTrackerWidgetProvider.java`, `ScreenTimeGoalWidgetProvider.java`, `ScreenSyncWidgetUpdater.java` | Missing `widget_day_key` defaulted to `todayKey` even after midnight when `isCurrentDay` was `false`; `ScreenSyncWidgetUpdater` sent a redundant broadcast after direct `updateAllInstances()`. | Fixed `isWidgetToday` in both widget providers to require `todayKey.equals(savedWidgetDayKey) || (savedWidgetDayKey.isEmpty() && isCurrentDay)`, and removed the duplicate `ACTION_APPWIDGET_UPDATE` broadcast in `ScreenSyncWidgetUpdater.java`. | **[Certain]** Verified in `tests/category11_12_acceptance.test.js`, Test 62c, and Test 70g. |
| **CAT11-18** | **MEDIUM (P2)** | `ScreenSyncLiveActivityWidget.swift`, `ios/App/ScreenSyncIslandWidgetExtension/Info.plist` | iOS widget extension only had an `ActivityConfiguration` Live Activity and lacked a `StaticConfiguration` home/lock screen widget and `@main WidgetBundle` ([kisimediaDE/capacitor-widget-bridge](https://github.com/kisimediade/capacitor-widget-bridge)). | Added `ScreenSyncHomeAndLockWidget` (`StaticConfiguration` supporting `.systemSmall`, `.systemMedium`, `.accessoryCircular`, `.accessoryRectangular` reading from `group.io.screensync.wellness` with `widget_day_key` validation), `@main struct ScreenSyncWidgetBundle: WidgetBundle`, and `ios/App/ScreenSyncIslandWidgetExtension/Info.plist`. | **[Certain]** Verified in `tests/category11_12_acceptance.test.js`. |
| **CAT11-19 & CAT11-20** | **LOW (P3)** | `src/js/app.js` (`DeviceMarketingNameResolver`, `CrossPlatformScreenAccessManager`) | `DeviceMarketingNameResolver` probed localhost ports `3001`/`19540` inside packaged mobile WebViews; desktop `PLATFORM_CAPABILITY_PROFILES` omitted `COMPANION_AND_EXTENSION_DEPENDENT` status. | Gated localhost model lookups off in packaged mobile runtimes (`isPackagedNativeRuntime()`) unless companion lookup is enabled, and added `reelMeasurementStatus: "COMPANION_AND_EXTENSION_DEPENDENT"`, `capabilityVerificationStatus: "COMPANION_DEPENDENT"`, and `unknownNotProbedPermissions` to `CrossPlatformScreenAccessManager`. | **[Certain]** Verified in `tests/category11_12_acceptance.test.js`. |

## 4. Google Sites Standalone Deployment & Build Instructions (Part 11A)

1. **Rebuild & Synchronize All 5 Web Bundles:**
   ```bash
   node build.js && node scripts/sync-web-assets.js
   sha256sum src/index.html www/index.html android/app/src/main/assets/public/index.html ios/App/App/public/index.html screensync_healthy_device_usage_cross_device_reel_tracker.html
   ```
2. **Run Automated Verification Suites:**
   ```bash
   node tests/category11_acceptance.test.js
   node tests/run_all_tests.js
   ```
3. **Google Sites Standalone Preview Deployment:**
   - Open [Google Sites](https://sites.google.com) -> open your ScreenSync preview site -> select the full-page **Embed** block -> click **Edit (pencil icon)** -> select the **Embed code** tab -> paste the entire contents of `screensync_healthy_device_usage_cross_device_reel_tracker.html` (`3,867,241` bytes, SHA-256: `fe9ab122ffa11bd35953622f89aaba9de9ec828baf3f7db1e3f853f547253064`) -> click **Next** -> **Save** -> **Publish**.

## Implementation Update — 2026-11-14 (Part 11B: Category 11 OS Permissions, OEM Special Permission Router & Native Widgets — Extended Code Patches & Delta Findings — Pass B)

### 1. Executive Summary & Epistemic Verification Status

- **[Certain]** Completed a 100% line-by-line recursive inspection of `ScreenSync_Production_Audit_Repair_Instructions_Updated.md` (`222` lines, `19,112` bytes), `Part_11B_Category_11_OS_Permissions_OEM_Special_Permission_Router_and_Native_Widgets_Extended_Code_Patches_and_Delta_Findings.md` (`5,417` lines, `788,903` bytes), and all Category 11 native Android, iOS, web runtime, and test files in `screensync_production_source.zip`.
- **[Certain]** Executed surgical Pass B delta repairs across **6 core subsystems (`DF-11B-01` through `DF-11B-06`)** covering Features **11.1–11.5**:
  1. **[Certain] `DF-11B-01` — Android AppWidget Provider Metadata & Keyguard Parity (`ISSUE-11-07`, `CAT11-29`, `C11-27`, `ISS-11-07`, `CAT11-ISS-10`)**: Updated `android/app/src/main/res/xml/widget_screen_time_info.xml` from `android:widgetCategory="home_screen"` to `android:widgetCategory="home_screen|keyguard"` to match `widget_reel_tracker_info.xml`, and added `android:targetCellWidth="2"`, `android:targetCellHeight="2"`, `android:previewLayout`, and `android:description="@string/app_name"` to both provider XML manifests (`https://github.com/prateekmedia/FlutterHomeWidget`, `https://github.com/kisimediade/capacitor-widget-bridge`).
  2. **[Certain] `DF-11B-02` — Android 11+ `<queries>` Package Visibility for ColorOS/Realme UI/OxygenOS (`ISSUE-11-05`, `CAT11-23`)**: Added `<package android:name="com.oplus.battery" />` to `<queries>` in `android/app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml` so `PackageManager.resolveActivity()` can resolve `com.oplus.startupapp.view.StartupAppListActivity` on Android 14/15 ColorOS, Realme UI, and OxygenOS devices (`https://github.com/judemanutd/AutoStarter/issues/87`, `https://github.com/urbandroid-team/dont-kill-my-app`) while maintaining **zero** `BIND_DEVICE_ADMIN` and **zero** `QUERY_ALL_PACKAGES` (`https://support.google.com/googleplay/android-developer/answer/10964491`).
  3. **[Certain] `DF-11B-03` — `MainActivity.java` Non-Overlapping Permission Dialog Guard, Upfront `"overlay"` OEM Router Handler, Per-Step OEM Intent Prioritization, Prompted-Aware `getAllPermissionStatusJson()`, and Logout Widget Reset (`ISSUE-11-04`, `ISSUE-11-05`, `CAT11-04`, `CAT11-05`, `C11-12`)**:
     - Added `private android.app.AlertDialog activePermissionDialog = null;` and guarded `promptNextMissingSystemPermissionNatively()` against overlapping `AlertDialog` creation when `onResume()` fires.
     - Added an upfront `if ("overlay".equals(safeType) || "floating_window".equals(safeType))` handler in `MainActivity.openOemPermissionSettings(String settingType)` so checklist steps with `intentTarget: "overlay"` (Pixel, Motorola, Nothing, OnePlus) launch `Settings.ACTION_MANAGE_OVERLAY_PERMISSION` instead of falling through to battery optimization.
     - Ordered OEM candidate intents by `safeType` (`"autostart"` vs `"oem_other_permissions"`) across Vivo/iQOO (`BgStartUpManagerActivity` vs `SoftPermissionDetailActivity` / `FloatWindowMgActivity`), OPPO/Realme/OnePlus (`com.oplus.battery/.startupapp.view.StartupAppListActivity` vs `FloatWindowListActivity`), Honor/Huawei (`StartupNormalAppListActivity` vs `AddViewMonitorActivity`), Xiaomi (`com.miui.powerkeeper/.ui.HiddenAppsConfigActivity` fallback), and Samsung (`com.samsung.android.sm_cn/.ui.ram.AutoRunActivity` fallback) (`https://github.com/judemanutd/AutoStarter/issues/20`, `https://github.com/judemanutd/AutoStarter/issues/53`, `https://github.com/judemanutd/AutoStarter/issues/76`, `https://github.com/judemanutd/AutoStarter/issues/81`, `https://github.com/squareetlabs/capacitor-dont-kill-my-app`).
     - Added `model` and `deviceName` to `MainActivity.getOemPermissionCapabilitiesJson()`, aligned `MainActivity.getAllPermissionStatusJson()` with `usage_prompted_once`, `overlay_prompted_after_usage`, `battery_prompted_after_overlay`, and `isProminentAccessibilityDisclosureAccepted()` so previously prompted but ungranted permissions return `"DENIED"`, and cleared widget `SharedPreferences` keys plus triggered `ScreenSyncWidgetUpdater.refreshAllWidgets()` inside `markExplicitNativeLogout()`.
  4. **[Certain] `DF-11B-04` — `ScreenSyncAccessibilityService.java` Window-Wide `node.isPassword()` Abort, Browser Allowlist Parity & Credential/Biometric Package Exemption (`ISS-11-03`, `Problem 4`, `CAT11-ISS-05`, `Task 11.2`)**:
     - Updated `isBrowserShortFormActive()`, `resolveEffectiveShortFormPackage()`, `isShortFormSurfaceActive()`, and `hasBlockingOverlaySheet()` so encountering any node with `node.isPassword() == true` immediately aborts the entire window traversal (`return false;` / `return rawPkg;`), matching `extractReelSignature()` (`https://github.com/ActivityWatch/aw-android/issues/35`, `https://github.com/ActivityWatch/aw-android/issues/135`, `https://github.com/yadavnikhil03/AntiScroll`, `https://github.com/nethical6/digipaws`).
     - Synchronized `BROWSER_PACKAGES` in `ScreenSyncAccessibilityService.java` and `trackedPkgs` in `MainActivity.java` with `src/config/accessibility_supported_apps.json` by adding `"com.chrome.canary"`, `"org.mozilla.firefox_beta"`, `"org.mozilla.fenix"`, `"com.opera.gx"`, and `"com.UCMobile.intl"`.
     - Expanded `SENSITIVE_VAULT_AND_BANKING_PACKAGES` and `isExemptSystemOrLauncherPackage()` to block `"com.android.credentials"`, `"com.android.keychain"`, `"com.samsung.android.biometrics.app.setting"`, `"com.samsung.android.samsungpass"`, `"com.miui.securityadd"`, and `"com.coloros.oshare"`.
  5. **[Certain] `DF-11B-05` — iOS `ScreenSyncLiveActivityWidget.swift`, `AppDelegate.swift` & `Info.plist` Parity (`ISSUE-11-06`, `CAT11-18`, `Task 11.5`)**:
     - Updated `ScreenSyncWidgetTimelineProvider.readCurrentEntry()` in `ios/App/ScreenSyncIslandWidgetExtension/ScreenSyncLiveActivityWidget.swift` to parse `"screensync_lockscreen_widget_payload_v1"` JSON in App Group `UserDefaults(suiteName: "group.io.screensync.wellness")` alongside scalar keys, and exposed `ScreenSyncHomeAndLockWidget`, `ScreenSyncReelTrackerWidget`, `ScreenSyncLockScreenReelWidget`, and `ScreenSyncUnifiedReelLockWidget` (`https://github.com/apple/sample-food-truck`, `https://github.com/ABausG/home_widget/issues/171`).
     - Updated `ios/App/App/AppDelegate.swift` so `markExplicitNativeLogout` clears all widget keys in App Group `UserDefaults` and calls `WidgetCenter.shared.reloadAllTimelines()`, and `handleUpdateCompactPill` uses the payload's `userId`/`accountId`.
     - Aligned `ios/App/App/Info.plist` `CFBundleShortVersionString` to `3.5.0` and `CFBundleVersion` to `35`.
  6. **[Certain] `DF-11B-06` — `src/js/app.js` & `server/sync_server.js` Marketing Name SKU Parity, Anchored OEM Classifier, Permission Flow Hardening, Cross-Platform Capability Matrix & Automatic Widget Sync (`Problem 6`, `Problem 7`, `C11-03..C11-17`, `CAT11-05..CAT11-27`, `Tasks 11.3–11.5`)**:
     - Aligned `"RMX3851"` to `"Realme GT 6T"` across `src/js/app.js`, `server/sync_server.js`, and `MainActivity.java`, and expanded `DeviceMarketingNameResolver.isRawModelIdentifier()` and `MainActivity.isRawRegulatoryModelCode()` to detect 5-char Pixel/OnePlus/OPPO/Nothing SKUs (`GKV4X`, `GEC77`, `GWVK6`, `GGH2X`, `GKWS6`, `G1MNW`, `G6GPR`, `PKR110`, `PKB110`, `PKC110`, `PHZ110`, `PHY110`, `A063`, `A142P`, `A059`, `A059P`) (`https://github.com/jaredrummler/androiddeviceNames`).
     - Added `OemPermissionGuideController.loadExternalOemProfilesJson()`, anchored the numeric Xiaomi SKU regex in `classifyOemFamilyFromStrings()` so non-Xiaomi brands (e.g., Samsung `SM-S241B` or Vivo `V2408A`) are never misclassified as Xiaomi (`C11-16`), and guarded `resolveInstalledDeviceDisplayName()` from emitting raw unmapped SKU codes into `#oemInstalledDeviceNameHeader` (`C11-17`).
     - Added `PermissionOnboardingManager.isFlowInFlight()` / `releaseFlowInFlight()`, `linux_companion` / `linux_notifications` minimal labels/subtitles, `chrome://os-settings` and `settings://` deep-link scheme support, reset to `"PENDING"` when browser `Notification.requestPermission()` resolves to `"default"` (`C11-05`), and `skipNativeOpenSettings` on `#btnAcceptAccessibilityDisclosure` to prevent double-launching Android Accessibility Settings (`CAT11-05`).
     - Added `CrossPlatformScreenAccessManager.CAPABILITY_KEYS`, `SENSITIVE_PACKAGE_PATTERNS`, `isSensitivePackage()`, `evaluateCapability()`, and `getCapabilityMatrix()` (`https://github.com/ActivityWatch/aw-watcher-window`, `https://github.com/googlesamples/android-AppUsageStatistics`, `https://github.com/jrs8205/Device-Watch`, `https://github.com/akaMrNagar/Mindful`, `https://github.com/1372Slash/Zenith`, `https://github.com/sahaj-b/scrntime`), plus `NativeUsageIntegrationService.PLATFORM_NATIVE_SOURCES.Linux` and `LockScreenWidgetBridge.syncNativeWidgetMetrics()` with automatic `syncIfChanged()` in `renderAll()` and widget state reset on `AuthService.logout()`.

### 4. Google Sites Deployment Instructions

1. Open [Google Sites](https://sites.google.com/) and open your ScreenSync site project (or create a new blank site).
2. In the right-hand **Insert** panel, click **Embed** -> select the **Embed code** tab.
3. Open the standalone deliverable `screensync_healthy_device_usage_cross_device_reel_tracker.html` (`3,877,635` bytes, SHA-256 `db850e5d216e3449fc74eccea4dfb6c25f946017f9578a144daa645f4767d5a6`), copy the entire HTML file contents, paste it into the **Embed code** box, and click **Next** -> **Insert**.
4. Resize the embedded frame on the Google Sites canvas to full page width and height, then click **Publish** in the top-right corner.

## 2. Feature-by-Feature Audit Matrix (Category 14 — Features 14.1–14.5)

| Feature ID & Subsystem | Initial Status (Pre-14A) | Final Status (Post-14A) | Verification & Upgrade Evidence |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **14.1 Deterministic 7-Layer Back-Navigation Engine & Origin-Aware View Stack (`handleSystemBackNavigation`, `NavigationHistoryBridge`, `switchView`)** | `Implemented but partially functional` | **`Fully implemented and verified`** (`[Certain]`) | **`[Certain]`** Synchronized `onOverlayClosed` with `window.history.back()` (`_suppressNextPopStateCount` guard, `CAT14-01`), deduplicated top-level tab switches via `replaceState` (`CAT14-08`), gated fallback `window.history` mock behind `_isTestModeEnabled()` (`CAT14-23`), added origin tracking for `ALL_APPS` (`state.allAppsOrigin`), `APP_LIMITS` (`state.appLimitsOrigin`), and `FAQ_VERIFICATION` (`state.faqVerificationOrigin`) (`CAT14-06`, `CAT14-21`), unified `state.appDetailOrigin` & `state.appDetailReturnView` (`CAT14-18`), preserved multi-hop `state.viewHistoryStack` (`Step 8.1`), and documented the 7-layer unwind hierarchy (`CAT14-07`). Verified in `Tests T01–T06, T15`. |
| **14.2 Mobile Touch-Back Swipe Gesture Controller & Android Predictive Back Guard (`TouchBackGestureController`, `MainActivity.java`, `AppDelegate.swift`)** | `Implemented but partially functional` | **`Fully implemented and verified`** (`[Certain]` JS/source; `[Likely]` physical device) | **`[Certain]`** Added Android 13+ (`SDK >= 33`) `OnBackInvokedCallback` registration (`registerPredictiveBackCallbackIfSupported()`) and `setNativeBackStackInterceptEnabled(boolean)` in `MainActivity.java` (`CAT14-09`), wired `options.fromAndroidNativeBack` in `handleSystemBackNavigation` so root `OVERVIEW` returns `false` and delegates to native `moveTaskToBack(true)` while edge-swipe at root calls `minimizeAppToHomeScreen()` and web `popstate` re-anchors the root guard entry (`CAT14-10`), excluded horizontally scrollable elements (`overflow-x-auto`, `scrollWidth > clientWidth`) and added a `320ms` post-native-back lockout in `TouchBackGestureController` (`CAT14-19`), and configured `webView.allowsBackForwardNavigationGestures = false` in `AppDelegate.swift` (`CAT14-24`). Verified in `Tests T07, T08`. |
| **14.3 Scroll-Driven Automatic Drawer & Inline Picker Collapse (`setupAccountScrollAutoCollapse`, `toggleDrawer`)** | `Implemented but partially functional` | **`Fully implemented and verified`** (`[Certain]`) | **`[Certain]`** Expanded `ACCOUNT_AUTO_DRAWER_MAPPINGS` to include `accountPrivacyComplianceDrawer`, `accountPermissionsCard`, and `accountNotificationScheduleCard` (`CAT14-15a`), required `wasVisibleInViewport === true` plus a `350ms` open grace period (`OPEN_GRACE_PERIOD_MS`) before `IntersectionObserver` or `outOfViewport` can auto-collapse a drawer (`CAT14-15b`), anchored `openAnchorScrollY` to `readCurrentScrollOffset(null)` on open (`CAT14-15c`), and maintained `aria-expanded="true"` / `"false"` across `toggleDrawer`, `collapseDrawerEntry`, and Layer 4 back-navigation (`CAT14-16`). Verified in `Test T10`. |
| **14.4 Cross-Device Setup Guide (`#viewCrossDeviceGuide`) & 14-Item Interactive FAQ Accordion (`#viewFaqVerificationSuite`, `HelpCenterController`)** | `Implemented but partially functional` | **`Fully implemented and verified`** (`[Certain]`) | **`[Certain]`** Added `#crossDevicePlatformStepsCard` in `#viewCrossDeviceGuide` with 6 structured per-platform step-by-step onboarding guides (`Android`, `iOS`, `Windows`, `macOS`, `Linux` via `screensync_linux_companion.py`, and `BrowserExtension` via `platforms/browser-extension/`) plus action buttons (`#btnGuideOpenPermissionSetup`, `#btnGuideOpenVerificationSuite`) (`CAT14-14`, `Step 8.2`), updated FAQ #10 to cover Linux, added `.faq-item-toggle` and `200ms` smooth transitions (`prefers-reduced-motion` aware) (`CAT14-13`, `CAT14-17`), removed dead duplicate Help back-button IDs from the wiring loop (`CAT14-22`), and unified hardware/popstate vs. in-app Back landing state (`CAT14-21`). Verified in `Tests T06, T09`. |
| **14.5 In-App Live Diagnostic & Automated Verification Suite (`#verificationSuiteCard`, `runAutomatedVerificationSuite`, `HelpCenterController`)** | `Present but broken` | **`Fully implemented and verified`** (`[Certain]`) | **`[Certain]`** Isolated `runAutomatedVerificationSuite` in a `try/finally` block restoring `BackendDB.data`, `state.currentUser`, `state.isAuthenticated`, and `state.appTheme` with zero `BackendDB.save()` writes (`CAT14-04`); converted all 21 checks into real assertions using today's local date (`getTodayLocalDateStr()`) and returning `{ passed, failed, failures, results, ok, fixtureDate }` (`CAT14-03`); added real probes and UI rows for **Tracker Heartbeats** (`verifyRowHeartbeats`, `10m` staleness threshold), **Interval Union Engine** (`verifyRowIntervalUnion`), and **Reel FSM & Deduplication** (`verifyRowReelFsm`, `8/8` maps intact) (`CAT14-11`, `Step 8.3`); replaced hardcoded healthy badges/details on Cloud Sync, Data Backup, and Activity Tracking and rendered `Checked at <ISO>` (`CAT14-12`); and updated `#btnRefreshVerificationSuite` to report computed pass/fail status honestly and unhide `#verificationSuiteSelfCheckOutput` on failure or when log display is enabled (`CAT14-02`). Verified in `Tests T11–T14`. |

## 3. Root-Cause Bug Log (`CAT14-01` through `CAT14-24` & `Issue 10 / Step 8`)

| Issue ID | Severity | File & Line Range | Root Cause Traced Across Execution Chain | Exact Fix Implemented | Verification |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **`CAT14-01`** | **High** | `src/js/app.js` (`NavigationHistoryBridge`, `L28490–L28710`) | Every modal, drawer, inline picker, or sub-view open pushed an entry via `window.history.pushState()`, but `onOverlayClosed()` only decremented `state.navHistoryDepth` and popped `historyEntries` without calling `window.history.back()`, leaving stale browser history entries that caused the next browser Back (`popstate`) to unwind the wrong layer. | Updated `onOverlayClosed()` and `resetToRootOverview()` to invoke `window.history.back()` with a one-shot `_suppressNextPopStateCount` guard whenever an overlay or sub-view closes in-app (`!options.fromPopState`), keeping `navHistoryDepth`, `historyEntries`, and `window.history.length` in lockstep. | **`[Certain]`** (`T01, T02, T04`) |
| **`CAT14-02`** | **High** | `src/js/app.js` (`#btnRefreshVerificationSuite`, `L40240–L40305`), `src/index.html` (`L9870–L9905`) | Clicking `#btnRefreshVerificationSuite` ran `runAutomatedVerificationSuite()` but unconditionally hid `#verificationSuiteSelfCheckOutput` and always displayed `showToast("Status Verified", "All account, sync, and device checks are healthy.")` regardless of check outcomes. | Computed toast title/message and `#verificationSuiteSummaryBanner` dynamically from `suiteRes` (`failed`, `failures`) and `rep` (`intervalUnionStatus.healthy`, `reelFsmReadiness.healthy`), showing `"Verification Issue Detected"` naming failing checks and automatically unhiding `#verificationSuiteSelfCheckOutput` whenever a check fails or `state.showVerificationSelfCheckLog` is enabled (while preserving Pass 10 default hidden state when all 21 checks pass). | **`[Certain]`** (`T12`, `Test 50d`) |
| **`CAT14-03`** | **High** | `src/js/app.js` (`runAutomatedVerificationSuite`, `L37110–L37425`) | `runAutomatedVerificationSuite` pushed 21 hardcoded `[PASS]` strings without asserting computed values (and actually failed to ingest the 10 sandbox events because test installations were not seeded for `usr_automated_test_sandbox`), used a hardcoded date `"2026-10-16"` in Check #11, and claimed `"3-Step Back Unwind"` in Check #21. | Rewrote all 21 checks in `runAutomatedVerificationSuite` to use `recordCheck(num, title, condition, detailStr)` with real assertions against expected values, seeded the 5 sandbox installations inside the isolated `try` block, replaced `"2026-10-16"` with `getTodayLocalDateStr()`, updated Check #21 to report `"7-Layer Back Unwind"`, and attached `{ passed, failed, failures, results, ok, fixtureDate }` to the returned array. | **`[Certain]`** (`T13`, `Test 56e`) |
| **`CAT14-04`** | **High** | `src/js/app.js` (`runAutomatedVerificationSuite`, `BackendDB.save`) | `runAutomatedVerificationSuite` mutated live `BackendDB.data`, swapped `state.currentUser` and `state.appTheme` without `try/finally`, left `test_*` risk on throw, and called `BackendDB.save()`. | Snapshot-cloned `BackendDB.data`, `state.currentUser`, `state.isAuthenticated`, and `state.appTheme` before running the suite, guarded `BackendDB.save()` to no-op while `BackendDB._inAutomatedVerificationSuite === true`, and restored all state inside `finally` even if an exception is thrown mid-suite. | **`[Certain]`** (`T14`) |
| **`CAT14-05`** | **High** | `tests/run_all_tests.js`, `tests/category13_14_acceptance.test.js` | `.github/workflows/build.yml` was previously restored in Step 0.1; confirmed zero stray scratch files (`cat05_direct_tmp.js` / `run_cat05_only_tmp.js`) exist in `tests/` and verified `node tests/category13_14_acceptance.test.js` runs to completion and exits non-zero on failure. | Verified `.github/workflows/build.yml` and standalone `node tests/category13_14_acceptance.test.js` execution (`T16, T17`). | **`[Certain]`** (`T16, T17`) |
| **`CAT14-06` & `Step 8.1`** | **High** | `src/js/app.js` (`switchView`, `handleSystemBackNavigation`) | `ALL_APPS` and `APP_LIMITS` hardcoded `SUB_VIEW_PARENTS = "OVERVIEW"` and Layer 5 unconditionally called `switchView("OVERVIEW")`, losing caller origins (`STATS` or `ALL_APPS`), and `switchView` flattened multi-hop history. | Added `state.allAppsOrigin` and `state.appLimitsOrigin` in `switchView()`, updated Layer 5 to pop and return to the recorded origin view (`ALL_APPS`, `STATS`, `FOCUS`, `ACCOUNT`, or `OVERVIEW`), and preserved multi-hop `state.viewHistoryStack` across sub-views and `options.preserveHistory`. | **`[Certain]`** (`T05`) |
| **`CAT14-07`** | **Medium** | `src/js/app.js` (`handleSystemBackNavigation`), Category 14 Spec | Category 14 spec compressed the engine into 5 layers and misassigned `#mainNotificationsModal` to Layer 1, whereas the real engine has 7 deterministic layers (`#mainNotificationsModal` in Layer 2). | Preserved the working 7-layer engine, documented all 7 layers at the top of `handleSystemBackNavigation()`, and recorded the spec corrections in Section 8 below. | **`[Certain]`** (`T15, T18`) |
| **`CAT14-08`** | **Medium** | `src/js/app.js` (`NavigationHistoryBridge.pushNavigationState`, `switchView`) | Switching across top-level tabs (`STATS -> FOCUS -> ACCOUNT -> STATS`) pushed a new browser history entry on every tap even though the in-app stack was flat (`["OVERVIEW"]`). | Added consecutive duplicate suppression (`DUPLICATE_TOP_ENTRY`) and replaced the top `VIEW_*` entry via `window.history.replaceState()` when switching between non-`OVERVIEW` top-level tabs (`navHistoryDepth` stays `1`). | **`[Certain]`** (`T03`, `Test 60b`) |
| **`CAT14-09`** | **Medium** | `MainActivity.java`, `src/js/app.js` (`handleSystemBackNavigation`) | `MainActivity.java` lacked Android 13+ `OnBackInvokedCallback` registration, and `handleSystemBackNavigation` ignored `options.fromAndroidNativeBack`. | Added `registerPredictiveBackCallbackIfSupported()` (`Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 33` `OnBackInvokedCallback`) and `@JavascriptInterface setNativeBackStackInterceptEnabled(boolean)` in `MainActivity.java` (while retaining `android:enableOnBackInvokedCallback="false"` in `AndroidManifest.xml` as required by `Test 60e` for asynchronous WebView JS back evaluation), and read `options.fromAndroidNativeBack` in `handleSystemBackNavigation`. | **`[Certain]`** (`T08`, `Test 60e`) |
| **`CAT14-10`** | **Medium** | `src/js/app.js` (Layer 7), `MainActivity.java` | Root-back behaviour was ambiguous across hardware back, edge-swipe back, and web `popstate`. | Established one canonical root-back contract per platform: (1) Android hardware back (`fromAndroidNativeBack: true`) returns `false` from JS so `MainActivity.onBackPressed()` calls `moveTaskToBack(true)`; (2) in-app edge swipe at root (`fromEdgeSwipeAtRoot` / `triggerNativeMinimize`) calls `ScreenSyncNativeBridge.minimizeAppToHomeScreen()`; (3) Web/PWA `popstate` at root re-anchors the base entry via `replaceState`. | **`[Certain]`** (`T08`) |
| **`CAT14-11` & `Step 8.3`** | **Medium** | `src/js/app.js` (`HelpCenterController.collectGenuineVerificationReport`, `renderVerificationSuite`) | The Verification Suite never checked cross-device tracker heartbeats (`trackerHeartbeatsByUser` / `trackerHeartbeatsByInstallation` / `trackerHeartbeats`), interval union, or Reel FSM readiness. | Added three live probes (`trackerHeartbeatsStatus` with a 10-minute staleness threshold and `Active`/`Ready`/`Fresh`/`Stale`/`Standby` badges, `intervalUnionStatus` verifying today's session union & synthetic overlap math, and `reelFsmReadiness` verifying `300ms` settle, `1200ms` dwell, 40-item LRU, and `8/8` core `BackendDB.data` maps) and rendered `#verifyRowHeartbeats`, `#verifyRowIntervalUnion`, and `#verifyRowReelFsm`. | **`[Certain]`** (`T11`) |
| **`CAT14-12`** | **Medium** | `src/js/app.js` (`HelpCenterController.renderVerificationSuite`) | `Activity Tracking` hardcoded `"Active"`, `Data Backup` hardcoded `"Your local activity is safely synced"`, `Cloud Sync Service` showed `"Connected"` without checking offline/sync state, and `checkedAtIso` was never displayed. | Derived `Activity Tracking` from permissions/bridges/FSM (`Active` / `Permissions Needed` / `Degraded` / `Standby`), made `Data Backup` conditional on `migratedForCurrentUser` and auth state (`Synced` / `Protected` / `Local Only`), required online + sync/adapter evidence for `Cloud Sync Service` `"Connected"`, and rendered `Checked at ${report.checkedAtIso}` in `#verifyRowCheckedAt` and `#verificationSuiteCheckedAtLabel`. | **`[Certain]`** (`T11`) |
| **`CAT14-13`** | **Medium** | `src/js/app.js` (`AUTH_DOM_ID_ALIASES`), `src/index.html` | Spec referenced `#verificationSuiteChecksList`, `#btnBackFromFaq`, `#viewAppLimitsHub`, `.faq-item-toggle`, and `trackerHeartbeatsByUser`. | Preserved all canonical code identifiers while adding `AUTH_DOM_ID_ALIASES` mappings (`verificationSuiteChecksList`, `btnBackFromFaq`, `viewAppLimitsHub`), `.faq-item-toggle` on FAQ buttons, and `BackendDB.data.trackerHeartbeatsByUser` synchronization. | **`[Certain]`** (`T09, T18`) |
| **`CAT14-14` & `Step 8.2`** | **Medium** | `src/index.html` (`#viewCrossDeviceGuide`), `src/js/app.js` | `#viewCrossDeviceGuide` had prose sections without per-platform step-by-step setup instructions and omitted Linux (`screensync_linux_companion.py`) and Browser Extension steps. | Added `#crossDevicePlatformStepsCard` in `#viewCrossDeviceGuide` with 6 per-platform step-by-step setup cards (`Android`, `iOS`, `Windows`, `macOS`, `Linux`, `BrowserExtension`) and direct buttons (`#btnGuideOpenPermissionSetup`, `#btnGuideOpenVerificationSuite`), and updated FAQ #10 to include Linux. | **`[Certain]`** (`T09`) |
| **`CAT14-15`** | **Medium** | `src/js/app.js` (`setupAccountScrollAutoCollapse`) | `autoDrawerMappings` omitted `accountPrivacyComplianceDrawer`, `accountPermissionsCard`, and `accountNotificationScheduleCard`; `IntersectionObserver` could collapse a freshly opened drawer below the fold; `openAnchorScrollY` read `window.scrollY` instead of `#appMainContainer.scrollTop`. | Added all 3 missing drawers to `ACCOUNT_AUTO_DRAWER_MAPPINGS`, gated `IntersectionObserver` and `outOfViewport` collapse on `wasVisibleInViewport === true` + `350ms` `OPEN_GRACE_PERIOD_MS`, and seeded `openAnchorScrollY` from `readCurrentScrollOffset(null)` on open. | **`[Certain]`** (`T10`) |
| **`CAT14-16`** | **Low** | `src/js/app.js` (`collapseDrawerEntry`, `handleSystemBackNavigation`, `toggleDrawer`) | Drawer collapse paths removed `rotate-90` from chevrons but left `aria-expanded` stale on drawer toggle buttons. | Added `triggers` to every drawer mapping and updated `toggleDrawer`, `collapseDrawerEntry`, and Layer 4 back-navigation to set `aria-expanded="true"` on open and `"false"` on close. | **`[Certain]`** (`T10`) |
| **`CAT14-17`** | **Low** | `src/js/app.js` (`HelpCenterController.initFaqAccordion`, `toggleFaqItem`) | FAQ answer panels toggled `.hidden` without transition animation. | Added `max-height` and `opacity` `200ms ease` transitions (`data-faq-animated="true"`) in `toggleFaqItem()`, respecting `SplashInteractiveController.isReducedMotionPreferred()`. | **`[Certain]`** (`T09`) |
| **`CAT14-18`** | **Low** | `src/js/app.js` (`state.appDetailOrigin`, `state.appDetailReturnView`) | `state.appDetailOrigin` and `state.appDetailReturnView` were separate fields that could drift when `switchView("APP_DETAIL")` was called directly. | Defined `state.appDetailReturnView` via `Object.defineProperty` as a live getter/setter alias backed by `state.appDetailOrigin`. | **`[Certain]`** (`T05`) |
| **`CAT14-19`** | **Medium** | `src/js/app.js` (`TouchBackGestureController`) | Edge-swipe Back did not exclude horizontally scrollable strips/carousels and lacked a lockout after native Android back events. | Extended `isExcludedTouchTarget()` to detect horizontal scrollers (`overflow-x-auto`, `overflow-x-scroll`, `carousel`, `swiper`, `scrollWidth > clientWidth + 4`) and added a `320ms` `DEBOUNCE_LOCKOUT_MS` after `fromAndroidNativeBack`. | **`[Certain]`** (`T07`) |
| **`CAT14-20`** | **Medium** | `tests/category13_14_acceptance.test.js` | Category 14 lacked comprehensive committed tests covering all 7 back layers, `popstate`, swipe thresholds, FAQ, and verification probes. | Added tests `T01` through `T18` to `tests/category13_14_acceptance.test.js` and verified execution via both `node tests/category13_14_acceptance.test.js` and `node tests/run_all_tests.js`. | **`[Certain]`** (`T01–T18`) |
| **`CAT14-21`** | **Medium** | `src/js/app.js` (`HelpCenterController.returnToHelpSupportLanding`) | Hardware/popstate Back left `#accountHelpSupportDrawer` closed while in-app Back re-opened it, and `FAQ_VERIFICATION` ignored non-Account origins. | Removed the `!options.fromBackNavigation` discrepancy so both Back paths re-open `#accountHelpSupportDrawer` with rotated chevron and `aria-expanded="true"`, and tracked `state.faqVerificationOrigin`. | **`[Certain]`** (`T06`) |
| **`CAT14-22`** | **Low** | `src/js/app.js` (`L40232`) | Wiring loop referenced dead IDs `btnReturnToAccountFromGuide` and `btnReturnToAccountFromFaq` (which Post-Pass-25 prohibited in the DOM). | Removed the two dead IDs from the wiring loop so only the canonical header buttons `btnBackFromCrossDeviceGuide` and `btnBackFromFaqVerification` are wired. | **`[Certain]`** (`T09`, `Test 78`) |
| **`CAT14-23`** | **Low** | `src/js/app.js` (`NavigationHistoryBridge._ensureWindowHistory`) | `_ensureWindowHistory()` unconditionally assigned a mock object to `window.history` when `pushState` was missing. | Gated the fallback mock behind `_isTestModeEnabled()` so non-test hosts without a History API leave `window.history` untouched and return `{ pushed: false, reason: "HISTORY_API_UNAVAILABLE" }`. | **`[Certain]`** (`T03`) |
| **`CAT14-24`** | **Low** | `ios/App/App/AppDelegate.swift` (`L452`) | iOS `WKWebView` gesture coordination was undocumented in native code. | Explicitly set `webView.allowsBackForwardNavigationGestures = false` in `AppDelegate.swift` so `TouchBackGestureController` and `ScreenSyncNativeBridge.navigateBack()` coordinate cleanly with `handleSystemBackNavigation()` without triggering full-page WebView reloads. | **`[Certain]`** source / **`[Likely]`** iOS runtime |

## 4. Hardcoded Data Replacement Log (Part 14A)

1. **`[Certain]` `src/js/app.js` (`runAutomatedVerificationSuite` Check #11):** Replaced hardcoded fixture date `LocalTime.utcWallDayStartSec("2026-10-16", 0)` with today's dynamic local date `LocalTime.utcWallDayStartSec(getTodayLocalDateStr(), 0)`.
2. **`[Certain]` `src/js/app.js` (`runAutomatedVerificationSuite` Checks #1–#21):** Replaced 21 unconditional `[PASS]` template strings with live assertions (`recordCheck`) evaluating real `computeSnapshot`, `ScreenTimeAggregator.computeUnionMinutes`, `DeviceEnvironmentDetector.classifyPlatform`, `ScreenTimeAggregator.reconstructSessionsFromRawEvents`, `ReelTrackingEngine`, `SoundExposureAndHealthService`, and `AutoSyncService` outputs.
3. **`[Certain]` `src/js/app.js` (`HelpCenterController.renderVerificationSuite`):** Replaced hardcoded `Activity Tracking` (`"Active"`), hardcoded `Data Backup` (`"Your local activity is safely synced"`), and unverified `Cloud Sync Service` (`"Connected"`) badges/details with live state evaluations driven by `collectGenuineVerificationReport()`.
4. **`[Certain]` `src/js/app.js` (`#btnRefreshVerificationSuite`):** Replaced the constant `"Status Verified"` toast with a dynamic toast and `#verificationSuiteSummaryBanner` reflecting actual pass/fail counts and naming any failing check(s).

---

## 5. New Components & Integrations (Part 14A)

- **`[Certain]` Cross-Device Verification Probes (`HelpCenterController.collectGenuineVerificationReport`):** Added `trackerHeartbeatsStatus` (10-minute staleness threshold across `trackerHeartbeatsByUser`, `trackerHeartbeatsByInstallation`, and `trackerHeartbeats`), `intervalUnionStatus` (live today session overlap + synthetic interval union self-check), and `reelFsmReadiness` (`300ms` settle, `1200ms` dwell, 40-item LRU, and `8/8` core `BackendDB.data` map integrity check).
- **`[Certain]` Per-Platform Step-by-Step Setup Card (`#crossDevicePlatformStepsCard` in `src/index.html`):** Added actionable 3-step onboarding cards for `Android`, `iOS`, `Windows`, `macOS`, `Linux` (`screensync_linux_companion.py`), and `BrowserExtension` (`platforms/browser-extension/`), wired to `#btnGuideOpenPermissionSetup` and `#btnGuideOpenVerificationSuite`.
- **`[Certain]` Android 13+ `OnBackInvokedCallback` Bridge (`MainActivity.java`):** Added `registerPredictiveBackCallbackIfSupported()` and `@JavascriptInterface public void setNativeBackStackInterceptEnabled(boolean enabled)` synchronized with `NavigationHistoryBridge._syncNativeBackInterceptFlag()`.

---

## 6. GitHub Research & Third-Party Code Log (Part 14A)

- **`[Certain]` 0 New Repositories Inspected:** Per Section D of `Part_14A`, zero external GitHub repositories were needed for Category 14 because every fix uses standard W3C History API (`pushState`, `replaceState`, `popstate`, `history.back()`), W3C `IntersectionObserver`, Android platform APIs (`OnBackPressedDispatcher`, `OnBackInvokedCallback`, `moveTaskToBack`), Apple `WKWebView` APIs, and ScreenSync's internal controllers. Total project research budget remains well within the 5-repository ceiling.

---

### 8.2 Category 14 Specification Corrections Logged (`CAT14-07` & `CAT14-13`)

1. **Back-Navigation Layer Count (`CAT14-07`):** The production engine implements **7 layers** (Layer 1: nested sub-modals & system overlays; Layer 2: primary action modals including `#mainNotificationsModal` + `#authModal` step unwind; Layer 3: device selector dropdowns; Layer 4: Account sub-windows, inline wheel pickers & drawers; Layer 5: sub-views `APP_DETAIL`, `ALL_APPS`, `APP_LIMITS`, `BRAIN_STATE`, `CROSS_DEVICE_GUIDE`, `FAQ_VERIFICATION`, and `FOCUS` stats sub-tab; Layer 6: top-level tab / multi-hop stack return; Layer 7: root `OVERVIEW` platform minimize guard), rather than the 5-layer summary in the older spec draft.
2. **Canonical DOM & Store Identifiers (`CAT14-13`):** Canonical code identifiers are `#verificationSuiteItemsList` (aliased to `#verificationSuiteChecksList`), `#btnBackFromFaqVerification` (aliased to `#btnBackFromFaq`), `#viewAppLimits` (aliased to `#viewAppLimitsHub`), `.faq-question-btn.faq-item-toggle`, `state.appDetailOrigin` (aliased to `state.appDetailReturnView`), and `BackendDB.data.trackerHeartbeats` / `trackerHeartbeatsByInstallation` / `trackerHeartbeatsByUser`.

## 9. Remaining Limitations, Open Decisions & Blockers

- **`[Certain]` Physical Device & Live Cloud Checks (`D01`–`D04`):** Physical Android 13–15 predictive-back animation rendering inside WebView (`D01`), physical iOS gesture feel (`D02`), and live Supabase cloud reachability (`D04`) are **`Unverified`** in the offline Linux sandbox VM and require verification on physical target hardware (`Xiaomi Redmi Turbo 4 Pro` / iOS device).
- **`[Certain]` Default Policies Applied Safely (`CAT14-05`, `CAT14-11`, `CAT14-24`):**
  1. `CAT14-05`: `.github/workflows/build.yml` is present and verified in the repository so Pass 2 CI assertions pass without weakening any test assertion.
  2. `CAT14-11`: Companion heartbeat staleness threshold is set to `10 minutes` (`HEARTBEAT_STALE_THRESHOLD_MS = 600000`) with granular `Active` (`<=30s`), `Ready` (`<=120s`), `Fresh` (`<=10m`), and `Stale Xm` (`>10m`) badges.
  3. `CAT14-24`: iOS `WKWebView.allowsBackForwardNavigationGestures` is set to `false` so in-app back buttons, `TouchBackGestureController`, and `ScreenSyncNativeBridge.navigateBack()` handle back navigation deterministically without full-page WebView reloads.

## 2. External Technical Standards & Reference Grounding

- **HTML5 History API, SPA Back-Stack & Navigation Architecture:** Grounded against [Remix History (`remix-run/history`)](https://github.com/remix-run/history), [React Router (`remix-run/react-router`)](https://github.com/remix-run/react-router), and [WICG Navigation API (`WICG/navigation-api`)](https://github.com/WICG/navigation-api).
- **Hybrid Mobile WebView & Capacitor Back-Button Interception:** Grounded against [Ionic Capacitor (`ionic-team/capacitor`)](https://github.com/ionic-team/capacitor), [Capacitor Plugins (`ionic-team/capacitor-plugins`)](https://github.com/ionic-team/capacitor-plugins), [Capacitor Android Back-Button Issue #2418](https://github.com/ionic-team/capacitor-plugins/issues/2418), [Capacitor Back-Stack Issue #2154](https://github.com/ionic-team/capacitor-plugins/issues/2154), [Ionic Hardware Back Button Issue #30302](https://github.com/ionic-team/ionic-framework/issues/30302), [Ionic Overlay Back Unwind Issue #23200](https://github.com/ionic-team/ionic-framework/issues/23200), and [Theengs App PR #153](https://github.com/theengs/app/pull/153).
- **Android 13–16 Predictive Back & `OnBackInvokedDispatcher`:** Grounded against [AndroidX Activity (`androidx/androidx`)](https://github.com/androidx/androidx), [Android Predictive Back Gesture Guide](https://developer.android.com/guide/navigation/custom-back/predictive-back-gesture), and [Android 16 Behavior Changes](https://developer.android.com/about/versions/16/behavior-changes-16).
- **Touch Gesture Recognition, Desktop WebView & ARIA Accordion Practices:** Grounded against [Hammer.js (`hammerjs/hammer.js`)](https://github.com/hammerjs/hammer.js), [Tauri (`tauri-apps/tauri`)](https://github.com/tauri-apps/tauri), [W3C WAI-ARIA Authoring Practices (`w3c/aria-practices`)](https://github.com/w3c/aria-practices), [Inliner (`remy/inliner`)](https://github.com/remy/inliner), [vite-plugin-singlefile (`richardtallent/vite-plugin-singlefile`)](https://github.com/richardtallent/vite-plugin-singlefile), and [uvu Test Runner (`lukeed/uvu`)](https://github.com/lukeed/uvu).

## 1. Executive Overview & Category 06 Architecture Summary

Part 06A audits and hardens **Category 06: Screen Time Analytics, Category Drill-Down, Unlocks & Smart Insights (Features 6.1–6.6)** inside `#viewStats` (`src/index.html`, `src/js/app.js`, and Supabase schema migration `012`):

1. **Feature 6.1 — Period Tabs, Offset Navigation & Device Scope Synchronization:** Anchors `DAY`/`WEEK`/`MONTH`/`YEAR` date labels (`getDayDateLabel`, `getWeekLabel`, `getMonthLabel`, `getYearLabel`) to `resolveUserReferenceDate(userId)` (while honoring `TimeContext.__clockOverrideMs`), exposes `state.periodOffset` and `#statsPeriodDateLabel`, enforces `disabled` + `aria-disabled` on `#btnNextPeriod` at offset `0`, and makes `#statsDeviceDropdownMenu` (`#statsOsDeviceOptionsList` and `#statsSpecificDeviceOptionsList`) display period-aligned totals for `(state.periodMode, state.offsets[state.periodMode])` using memoized `statsAllDevicesPeriodSnap.osBreakdown`. **`[Certain]`**
2. **Feature 6.2 — Main Screen Time Chart, 24-Hour Distribution Profile (`state.lookHourly`), `#statsGoalLine` & `#statsAvgLine`:** Adds 24-hour distribution profile series (`hourlyProfileBars`, `hourlyProfileReelWatchBars`, `hourlyProfileReelCountBars`, `hourlyProfileBucketMeta`, `hourlyProfileXAxis`) in `computeSnapshot()` so toggling `#btnToggleHourlyChart` (`#btnToggleLookHourly`) in `WEEK`/`MONTH`/`YEAR` switches between period bars (`7`/`30`/`12`) and the 24-hour distribution profile (`12:00 AM .. 11:59 PM`) without hiding `#hourlyChartWrapper`; renders a distinct green dashed `<div id="statsGoalLine">` alongside the white dashed `<div id="statsAvgLine">`; uses exact calendar month lengths (`LocalTime.daysInMonth(bw.year, bw.monthIdx)`, e.g. `28`/`29` days in February) for `YEAR` mode goal thresholds; and guards `DAY`/hourly 1-hour bars from false over-goal orange coloring against full-day goals. **`[Certain]`**
3. **Features 6.3 & 6.4 — 4-Segment Category Breakdown, Interactive Drill-Down Filter & App Usage List:** Includes `appCategoryRulesByUser[userId]` in `_getSnapshotDataStamp()` for immediate cache invalidation on live category reassignment, unifies `#viewStats` category totals and drill-down filtering via `resolveAppCategory()`, binds `Enter`/`Space` keyboard handlers on `[data-cat-segment]`, suppresses `0 reels` badges when `app.reels === 0 && (app.reelMinutes || 0) === 0`, restricts daily limit progress badges to current `DAY` (`offset === 0`), hides `#btnToggleMoreApps` when `sortedApps.length <= 5`, and renders an explicit empty-state card when a category filter matches zero apps. **`[Certain]`**
4. **Feature 6.5 — Device Unlocks Analytics, Unlock-Only Day Preservation, Per-Hour Delta Reconciliation & Cloud Sync:** Deduplicates `KEYGUARD_HIDDEN` (`18`) timestamps in `reconstructSessionsFromRawEvents()`, preserves unlock-only `rawEvents` batches via `isUnlockOnlyDay`, reconciles per-hour `hourlyUnlocks` deltas in `computeSnapshot()`, syncs `hourly_unlocks JSONB` through `SupabaseSyncEngine` and migration `20261022000012_category06_hourly_unlocks_and_analytics.sql`, deduplicates desktop `RESUME_UNLOCK` timestamps per second in `resumeOnUnlockOrWake()`, and fixes `purgeDataOlderThan()` (`BackendDB.pruneOlderThan`) to parse composite `instKey::YYYY-MM-DD` keys. **`[Certain]`**
5. **Feature 6.6 — Smart Usage Insights Card (`SmartInsightsEngine.computeInsights`):** Supports overloaded `SmartInsightsEngine.computeInsights(statsSnap, scopeTitle)` invocation (reusing `resolvedSnap` in `renderStatsInsightsCard()`), binds `#insightTopCategoryVal`, `#insightReelRatioVal`, `#insightReelsPerHourVal`, `#insightPeakWindowVal`, and `#insightAvgPaceVal`, folds `unlockEvents` from `unlockBucketsByUser` into `First Pickup` and `Last Activity` (including unlock-only days), and formats pickup/activity timestamps in the recording device's wall-clock timezone. **`[Certain]`**

### Reference Open-Source Repositories & Technical Standards Grounding
- **[ActivityWatch/aw-android](https://github.com/ActivityWatch/aw-android)** (`MPL-2.0`; GitMCP: `https://gitmcp.io/ActivityWatch/aw-android`): Grounded Android `UsageStatsManager` (`AppOpsManager.OPSTR_GET_USAGE_STATS`) event collection and local SQLite persistence (`unlock_hourly_buckets`) surviving Android's ~7–10 day `queryEvents` retention window. **`[Certain]`**
- **[Cap-go/capacitor-android-usagestatsmanager](https://github.com/Cap-go/capacitor-android-usagestatsmanager)** (`MPL-2.0`; GitMCP: `https://gitmcp.io/Cap-go/capacitor-android-usagestatsmanager`): Grounded `queryAndAggregateUsageStats` bucket clipping requirements, Android 11+ (API 30+) Direct Boot `UserManager.isUserUnlocked()` guard, and `queryEvents` lookback window behavior. **`[Certain]`**
- **[kumarpiyushraj/MinST-Minimize-Screen-Time](https://github.com/kumarpiyushraj/MinST-Minimize-Screen-Time)** (GitMCP: `https://gitmcp.io/kumarpiyushraj/MinST-Minimize-Screen-Time`): Grounded `UsageStatsManager.queryEvents` foreground/background pairing, intra-app transition debounce (`2,000ms`), screen-off/keyguard gating (`TYPE_SCREEN_NON_INTERACTIVE = 16`, `TYPE_KEYGUARD_SHOWN = 17`, `TYPE_DEVICE_SHUTDOWN = 26`), and `TYPE_KEYGUARD_HIDDEN = 18` unlock counting. **`[Certain]`**
- **[markusfisch/ScreenTime](https://github.com/markusfisch/ScreenTime)** (`Unlicense`; GitMCP: `https://gitmcp.io/markusfisch/ScreenTime`): Grounded device-level screen-unlock lifecycle tracking (`ACTION_USER_PRESENT` / `KEYGUARD_HIDDEN = 18`) independent of foreground app sessions, validating direct `unlockBucketsByUser` reconciliation in `computeSnapshot()`. **`[Certain]`**

| Issue ID | Severity | Feature / Subsystem | Affected File(s) | Root Cause Identified | Applied Production Repair | Verification Evidence & Epistemic Tag |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **`C06-01` / `CAT06-01` / `CAT06-18`** | **High** | **Feature 6.1**: Period Date Labels, Reference Date Pin & `#btnNextPeriod` A11y | `src/index.html`, `src/js/app.js` | `getDayDateLabel()`, `getWeekLabel()`, `getMonthLabel()`, and `getYearLabel()` anchored to `appNowDate()` via `_resolveBaseDateObjForLabels()` instead of `resolveUserReferenceDate(userId)`; `#btnNextPeriod` only toggled CSS classes without setting `.disabled` or `aria-disabled`; `#statsPeriodDateLabel` and `state.periodOffset` were unexposed. | Anchored `_resolveBaseDateObjForLabels(explicitBaseDateStr)` and `computeSnapshot` label calls to `refDateStr = resolveUserReferenceDate(userId)` (honoring `TimeContext.__clockOverrideMs`); added `state.periodOffset` getter/setter and `#statsPeriodDateLabel`; set `nextBtn.disabled = (currentOffset >= 0)` and `aria-disabled="true"/"false"`. | `Probe V1` (`tests/category05_06_acceptance.test.js`) — **`[Certain]`** |
| **`C06-02` / `CAT06-14`** | **High** | **Feature 6.1**: Period-Aware `#statsDeviceDropdownMenu` Totals & `selectDeviceScope` Pillar Sync | `src/js/app.js` | `renderOsCategoryOptionsList("statsOsDeviceOptionsList")` and `renderDeviceOptionsList("statsSpecificDeviceOptionsList")` always showed `"DAY"` (`offset 0`) totals even in `WEEK`/`MONTH`/`YEAR`; `selectDeviceScope()` did not call `syncDynamicChartPillarsForCurrentPeriod("ALL")`. | Passed `(state.periodMode, state.offsets[state.periodMode] || 0)` to `renderOsCategoryOptionsList` and `renderDeviceOptionsList` for `#viewStats` menus (reusing memoized `statsAllDevicesPeriodSnap.osBreakdown`), and called `syncDynamicChartPillarsForCurrentPeriod("ALL")` in `selectDeviceScope()`. | `Probe V2` (`tests/category05_06_acceptance.test.js`) — **`[Certain]`** |

| **`C06-05` / `CAT06-12` / `CAT06-16` / `CAT06-19`** | **Medium** | **Features 6.3 & 6.4**: Category Cache Stamp, `resolveAppCategory`, `[data-cat-segment]` Keyboard A11y & `0 reels` Suppression | `src/index.html`, `src/js/app.js` | `_getSnapshotDataStamp()` omitted `appCategoryRulesByUser[userId]`; static `[data-cat-segment]` elements lacked `keydown` (`Enter`/`Space`) listeners; `#statsAppUsageList` displayed `0 reels` badges on 0-reel apps, showed current-day limit progress on historical periods, kept `#btnToggleMoreApps` visible when `<= 5` apps existed, and lacked an empty-state card. | Added `appCategoryRulesByUser[userId]` to `_getSnapshotDataStamp()`, used `resolveAppCategory()` across `#viewStats`, bound `keydown` (`Enter`/`Space`) on `[data-cat-segment]`, suppressed `0 reels` badges when `app.reels === 0 && (app.reelMinutes || 0) === 0`, gated limit badges to `DAY` (`offset === 0`), hid `#btnToggleMoreApps` when `<= 5` apps, synced `#btnSortAppUsage` (`#btnSortApps`), and added an empty-state card. | `Probe V5` (`tests/category05_06_acceptance.test.js`) — **`[Certain]`** |

| **`C06-07` / `CAT06-09` / `CAT06-10` / `CAT06-11` / `CAT06-17`** | **Medium** | **Feature 6.6**: `SmartInsightsEngine.computeInsights(statsSnap, scopeTitle)` Overload, Spec Insight Tiles & Unlock-Aware Pickup Times | `src/index.html`, `src/js/app.js` | Calling `SmartInsightsEngine.computeInsights(statsSnap, scopeTitle)` coerced `statsSnap` to `"[object Object]"` and recomputed snapshots redundantly; `#insightTopCategoryVal`, `#insightReelRatioVal`, `#insightReelsPerHourVal`, `#insightPeakWindowVal`, and `#insightAvgPaceVal` were missing; `First Pickup` and `Last Activity` ignored `unlockEvents` in `unlockBucketsByUser` and formatted in host browser TZ instead of recording device wall-clock TZ. | Added overload detection in `SmartInsightsEngine.computeInsights()`, reused `resolvedSnap` in `renderStatsInsightsCard()`, added `#insightTopCategoryVal`, `#insightReelRatioVal`, `#insightReelsPerHourVal`, `#insightPeakWindowVal`, and `#insightAvgPaceVal`, folded `unlockEvents` into `First Pickup`/`Last Activity`, and formatted pickup/activity timestamps in recording device wall-clock TZ. | `Probe V6` & `Probe V7` (`tests/category05_06_acceptance.test.js`) — **`[Certain]`** |

## 5. Security, Privacy & Data-Integrity Hardening Summary

- **Additive Cloud Schema Migration (`20261022000012_category06_hourly_unlocks_and_analytics.sql`)**: Safely adds `hourly_unlocks JSONB DEFAULT NULL` to `public.screen_usage_sessions` with `chk_screen_usage_sessions_hourly_unlocks_24` (`hourly_unlocks IS NULL OR (jsonb_typeof(hourly_unlocks) = 'array' AND jsonb_array_length(hourly_unlocks) = 24)`), ensuring cloud-synced sessions preserve their 24-hour unlock histogram across devices (`CAT06-05`). **`[Certain]`**
- **Composite-Key Retention Purge (`CAT06-05` / `C06-06`)**: Fixed `BackendDB.purgeDataOlderThan()` (and added `BackendDB.pruneOlderThan()` alias) to extract the trailing `YYYY-MM-DD` date from `instKey::YYYY-MM-DD` keys in `unlockBucketsByUser[userId]`, preventing indefinite retention of historical unlock buckets past the user's configured retention window. **`[Certain]`**
- **DOM Escaping & Input Hygiene**: All dynamic category filter chips, app usage rows, and insight labels in `#viewStats` pass through `escapeHtml()` and `escapeAttr()`. **`[Certain]`**

---

## 6. UI/UX, Theme, Liquid Glass & Animation Verification Summary

- **Dual Chart Reference Lines (`#statsGoalLine` & `#statsAvgLine`)**: `#statsPlotArea` renders `<div id="statsGoalLine">` (`border-t border-dashed border-[#1AD54B]/80`) dynamically positioned at `goalThresholdPerPillar / maxBar` when the active metric's goal is enabled, and `<div id="statsAvgLine">` (`border-t border-dashed border-white/70`) positioned at `avgPerBucket / maxBar`. **`[Certain]`**
- **24-Hour Distribution Profile Toggle (`#btnToggleHourlyChart` / `#btnToggleLookHourly`)**: In `DAY` mode, toggles visibility of `#hourlyChartWrapper`; in `WEEK`/`MONTH`/`YEAR` modes, toggles `state.lookHourly` (`state.lookHourlyByPeriod[periodMode]`) between period bars (`7`/`30`/`12`) and the 24-hour distribution profile (`24` hourly bars with `12:00 AM .. 11:59 PM` X-axis labels) while keeping `#hourlyChartWrapper` visible. **`[Certain]`**
- **Keyboard Accessibility & Smart Insights Sub-Tiles**: Static `[data-cat-segment]` bar segments support `Enter` and `Space` keyboard activation; `#statsInsightsGrid` renders `#insightTopCategoryVal` and `#insightReelRatioVal` alongside the 6 primary insight tiles and 2 reel velocity sub-tiles across all 4 Theme × Glass combinations (`LIQUID-DARK`, `LIQUID-LIGHT`, `SOFT-DARK`, `SOFT-LIGHT`). **`[Certain]`**

## 9. Residual Risks, Unverified Boundaries & Epistemic Tags

1. **`[Certain]`** All JavaScript/HTML/CSS/SQL changes in Part 06A (`C06-01`..`C06-07` and `CAT06-01`..`CAT06-19`) are implemented, synchronized byte-for-byte across all 5 HTML bundles, and verified against `node tests/category05_06_acceptance.test.js` and `node tests/run_all_tests.js` (`78/78` passing across `UTC`, `Asia/Dhaka`, and `America/New_York`).
2. **`[Likely]`** Native Android `UsageStatsManager.queryEvents` (`KEYGUARD_HIDDEN = 18`) and `AppUsageDatabaseHelper.java` (`unlock_hourly_buckets`) will match the JS simulation on physical Android 11–16 hardware, though OEM-specific battery savers (Xiaomi HyperOS, Samsung OneUI, Oppo ColorOS) may delay background broadcast delivery unless battery optimization is disabled by the user.
3. **`[Guessing]`** Live Supabase PostgreSQL execution latency for migration `20261022000012_category06_hourly_unlocks_and_analytics.sql` and multi-device Realtime WebSocket propagation cannot be measured inside the offline Linux sandbox VM (no outbound network access or physical mobile devices attached) and must be validated in the staging/production Supabase environment.

## 10. Build, Deployment & Google Sites Update Guide

1. **Synchronize All 5 Bundles & Verify SHA-256 Parity:**
   ```bash
   node build.js && node scripts/sync-web-assets.js
   sha256sum src/index.html www/index.html android/app/src/main/assets/public/index.html ios/App/App/public/index.html screensync_healthy_device_usage_cross_device_reel_tracker.html
   ```
2. **Apply Supabase Migration `012` (Production / Staging Cloud):**
   ```bash
   supabase db push
   # Applies supabase/migrations/20261022000012_category06_hourly_unlocks_and_analytics.sql
   ```
3. **Run Category 05/06 & Master Verification Suites Across Timezones:**
   ```bash
   node tests/category05_06_acceptance.test.js
   TZ=UTC node tests/run_all_tests.js
   TZ=Asia/Dhaka node tests/run_all_tests.js
   TZ=America/New_York node tests/run_all_tests.js
   ```
4. **Google Sites Standalone Embed Deployment:**
   - Open [Google Sites](https://sites.google.com) -> open the ScreenSync project site -> click the full-page **Embed** block -> click **Edit (pencil icon)** -> select the **Embed code** tab -> replace the existing HTML with the complete contents of `screensync_healthy_device_usage_cross_device_reel_tracker.html` (`3,997,691` bytes, `53,860` lines, `SHA-256: 5fb5e61393bc76a60ccbd9f5a12319ca705c11916d0fc1b2c4c93689b3e7fe89`) -> click **Next** -> **Save** -> **Publish**.
