### 1. Executive Summary & Synchronization Digest
- **Scope Executed**: Completed 100% line-by-line audit and production code implementation of `Part_06B_Category_06_Screen_Time_Analytics_Category_Drilldown_Unlocks_and_Smart_Insights_Extended_Code_Patches_and_Delta_Findings.md` (`3,541` lines, `556,388` bytes) across the `ScreenSync — Healthy Device Usage & Cross-Device Reel Tracker` (`io.screensync.wellness`, Version `3.5.0`, Build `35`) codebase `[Certain]`.
- **5-Bundle Byte-for-Byte SHA-256 Parity**: Rebuilt and synchronized all 5 HTML bundles (`src/index.html`, `www/index.html`, `android/app/src/main/assets/public/index.html`, `ios/App/App/public/index.html`, and `screensync_healthy_device_usage_cross_device_reel_tracker.html`) via `node build.js && node scripts/sync-web-assets.js`, producing an identical SHA-256 digest of `92016167658204f7415ca67d68bd145e55c0eb7cf39b4b67d3cf94aa2bd6b355` (`MD5: a9515456ac05186bba950d49ea351d5d`, `4,015,564` bytes, `54,170` lines) `[Certain]`.
- **Multi-Timezone Regression & Behavioral Acceptance Verification**: Executed `node tests/category05_06_acceptance.test.js` (including new Part 06B regression `Probes V8–V14`) and `node tests/run_all_tests.js` (`78/78` test suites) across `TZ=UTC`, `TZ=Asia/Dhaka`, and `TZ=America/New_York` with a **100% pass rate (exit code `0`)** and warm `renderAll()` latency of `36.64ms–39.80ms` (`< 50ms` budget) on the `2 installations × 90 days` benchmark fixture `[Certain]`.

### 2. Root-Cause Resolution Matrix (`CAT06-BUG-01` Through `CAT06-BUG-14` & `ISSUE-C06-05` / `ISSUE-C06-09`)

| Finding ID | Subsystem & File Locations | Root Cause & Production Fix Implemented | Verification Probe | Status |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **`CAT06-BUG-01` / `ISSUE-C06-09`** | `platforms/browser-extension/background_collector.js` (`line 408`), `src/js/app.js` (`BackendDB.recordDeduplicatedUnlockEvents`, `NativeUsageIntegrationService.ingestNativeUsageReport`, `CrossPlatformIdleAndSessionTracker.resumeOnUnlockOrWake`, `computeSnapshot`) | **Root Cause**: (1) `backfillHistoricalBrowserUsage30Days()` hardcoded `unlocks: 1` per day for `chrome.history` visits (`[Certain]`). (2) Multi-sync `rawEvents` batches assigned `unlocks` and `hourlyUnlocks` only to `appIdx === 0`, which was skipped when `records[0]` had `0` net new minutes while a later record in the batch had new usage (`[Certain]`). (3) Per-app `opens` and `notifs` in `rawEvents` were not delta-deduplicated against existing sessions on the same `(installation, localDate)` (`[Certain]`). **Fix**: Changed browser extension backfill to `unlocks: null`; added `BackendDB.recordDeduplicatedUnlockEvents()` keyed by millisecond timestamps; computed net deltas for `unlocks`, `hourlyUnlocks`, `opens`, and `notifs` across multi-sync batches and assigned batch unlock deltas to the first record actually ingested; wired `pendingUnlocksCount` in `CrossPlatformIdleAndSessionTracker`; and reconciled `hourlyUnlocks` deltas in `computeSnapshot()` (`[Certain]`). Grounded in [Android UsageEvents.Event](https://developer.android.com/reference/android/app/usage/UsageEvents.Event), [ActivityWatch/aw-android](https://github.com/ActivityWatch/aw-android) ([PR #380](https://www.google.com/search?q=https://github.com/ActivityWatch/aw-android/pull/380)), and [abrignoni/ALEAPP wellbeing.py](https://github.com/abrignoni/ALEAPP/blob/main/scripts/artifacts/wellbeing.py) `[Certain]`. | `Probes V6 & V8` (`tests/category05_06_acceptance.test.js`) | **100% Fixed & Verified** `[Certain]` |
| **`CAT06-BUG-02` & `CAT06-BUG-03` / `ISSUE-C06-05`** | `src/index.html` (`lines 7017, 7071–7082`), `src/js/app.js` (`AUTH_DOM_ID_ALIASES`, `renderAll`, `SmartInsightsEngine.computeInsights`) | **Root Cause**: `#statsMainGreyCard` lacked dedicated `#statsQuickGoalPillsRow` buttons (`#btnOpenScreenGoalFromStats` and `#btnOpenWatchGoalFromStats`), `#statsDeviceDropdownMenu` lacked `#statsAllDevicesMenuTotal` for the `"ALL"` scope option, and `AUTH_DOM_ID_ALIASES` lacked `insightReelVelocityVal` / `insightReelsVelocityVal` (`[Certain]`). **Fix**: Added `#statsQuickGoalPillsRow` with `#btnOpenScreenGoalFromStats` (`#statsQuickScreenGoalVal`) and `#btnOpenWatchGoalFromStats` (`#statsQuickWatchGoalVal`) inside `#statsMainGreyCard` wired to `openGoalModalFor("SCREEN", "STATS")` and `openGoalModalFor("REEL", "STATS")`; added `#statsAllDevicesMenuTotal` dynamically showing `All Authorized • <period total>`; and added `insightReelsVelocityVal` / `insightReelVelocityVal` aliases plus `reelsPerHourVelocityLabel` on `computeInsights()` (`[Certain]`). | `Probe V9` (`tests/category05_06_acceptance.test.js`) | **100% Fixed & Verified** `[Certain]` |
| **`CAT06-BUG-04`** | `src/js/app.js` (`renderAll` category section & donut, `init` `[data-cat-segment]` listener) | **Root Cause**: Clicking `#mainCatBarSocial` on `#viewOverview` set `state.selectedCategorySegment = "SOCIAL"` and called `switchView("STATS")`, after which the unscoped global `document.querySelectorAll("[data-cat-segment]")` listener fired on the same element and toggled `state.selectedCategorySegment` back to `null` (`[Certain]`). Additionally, `#statsCategoryDonutBox` only rendered its SVG in `DETAILED` graph mode (`[Certain]`). **Fix**: Excluded `#viewOverview` (`mainCat*`) elements from the Stats category toggle listener, synced `aria-pressed` and `data-selected` across all `[data-cat-segment]` elements, preserved keyboard focus on `Enter`/`Space`, and rendered `buildCategoryDonutSvg(...)` in `#statsCategoryDonutBox` across all graph styles (`SIMPLE`, `STANDARD`, `DETAILED`) (`[Certain]`). Grounded in [d3/d3-shape](https://github.com/d3/d3-shape) and [ManNguyen/LargestRemainderRound](https://github.com/ManNguyen/LargestRemainderRound) `[Certain]`. | `Probes V5 & V10` (`tests/category05_06_acceptance.test.js`) | **100% Fixed & Verified** `[Certain]` |
| **`CAT06-BUG-05` & `CAT06-BUG-06`** | `src/js/app.js` (`SmartInsightsEngine.computeInsights`) | **Root Cause**: When a user had short-form reel events (`reelEventsByUser`) earlier or later than general app sessions on a given day/period, `First Pickup` and `Last Activity` only scanned `scopedPeriodSessions` and `unlockBucketsByUser`, omitting `scopedPeriodReels` (`[Certain]`). **Fix**: Folded `scopedPeriodReels` (`startEpochSec`, `endEpochSec`, `watchSeconds`) into `earliestStartSec` and `latestEndSec` in `SmartInsightsEngine.computeInsights()` (`[Certain]`). Grounded in [ActivityWatch/aw-webui queries.ts](https://github.com/ActivityWatch/aw-webui/blob/master/src/queries.ts) `[Certain]`. | `Probes V7 & V11` (`tests/category05_06_acceptance.test.js`) | **100% Fixed & Verified** `[Certain]` |
| **`CAT06-BUG-07`** | `src/js/app.js` (`closeAllDeviceDropdownMenus`, `handleOutsideDeviceDropdownDismiss`) | **Root Cause**: Dismissing `#statsDeviceDropdownMenu` or `#deviceDropdownMenu` via outside click, scroll, `Escape`, or selecting a device hid the menu DOM node without calling `NavigationHistoryBridge.onOverlayClosed(menuId)`, stranding a phantom overlay entry on `NavigationHistoryBridge.historyEntries` (`[Certain]`). **Fix**: Updated `closeAllDeviceDropdownMenus(reason)` to check whether each menu was open and invoke `NavigationHistoryBridge.onOverlayClosed(menuId)` whenever `reason` is not `"backNavigation"` or `"popstate"`, and routed `handleOutsideDeviceDropdownDismiss` through `closeAllDeviceDropdownMenus("outside")` (`[Certain]`). | `Probe V12` (`tests/category05_06_acceptance.test.js`) | **100% Fixed & Verified** `[Certain]` |
| **`CAT06-BUG-09`, `CAT06-BUG-12` & `CAT06-BUG-14`** | `src/index.html` (`#unlocksPlotArea`), `src/js/app.js` (`[data-stats-pillar]`, `[data-unlocks-pillar]`, `#dayOfWeekButtons`, `#btnDrillToDay`) | **Root Cause**: `#unlocksChartWrapper` passed `null` as `plotAreaEl` to `positionChartTooltipWithinBounds()`; chart pillars lacked `aria-label` attributes, hover preview (`pointerenter`/`pointerleave`), and post-render keyboard focus retention on `ArrowLeft`/`ArrowRight`; and `#btnDrillToDay` did not synchronize `state.periodOffset` and `state.lookHourly` (`[Certain]`). **Fix**: Added `id="unlocksPlotArea"` in `src/index.html` and passed it to `positionChartTooltipWithinBounds()`; added `aria-label`, `pointerenter`/`pointerleave` tooltip preview, and keyboard focus retention on `[data-stats-pillar]`, `[data-unlocks-pillar]`, and `[data-week-day-idx]`; and synchronized `state.periodOffset` and `state.lookHourly = true` in `#btnDrillToDay` (`[Certain]`). Grounded in [floating-ui/floating-ui detectOverflow.ts](https://www.google.com/search?q=https://github.com/floating-ui/floating-ui/blob/master/packages/core/src/detectOverflow.ts%23L107-L124) and [recharts/recharts Issue #730](https://github.com/recharts/recharts/issues/730) `[Certain]`. | `Probes V13` (`tests/category05_06_acceptance.test.js`) | **100% Fixed & Verified** `[Certain]` |
| **`CAT06-BUG-10`, `CAT06-BUG-11` & `CAT06-BUG-13`** | `src/js/app.js` (`#statsAppUsageList`, `#btnSortAppUsage`, `#btnToggleMoreApps`, `#goalCurrentUsedText`, `#reelGoalCurrentUsedText`) | **Root Cause**: `#statsAppUsageList` did not filter out `a.key === "launcher"`, lacked secondary tie-breakers when `a.minutes === b.minutes`, omitted the `"Limited on iOS"` badge for reel-capable apps under iOS scope with 0 reels, and kept `#goalCurrentUsedText` / `#reelGoalCurrentUsedText` frozen to `Today` when inspecting `Yesterday` (`currentOffset < 0`) in `DAY` mode (`[Certain]`). **Fix**: Filtered `a.key !== "launcher"` from `#statsAppUsageList`, added deterministic tie-breaking (`minutes -> seconds -> reels -> localeCompare`), set `data-sort-mode` on `#btnSortAppUsage` and `aria-expanded` on `#btnToggleMoreApps`, rendered `"Limited on iOS"` badge when `isStatsIosScope && appSupportsReels && !showAppReelMetrics`, and bound `dayScreenUsed` / `dayReelUsed` to `statsSnap` when `state.periodMode === "DAY" && currentOffset < 0` (`[Certain]`). Grounded in [Cap-go/capacitor-android-usagestatsmanager](https://github.com/Cap-go/capacitor-android-usagestatsmanager) and [kumarpiyushraj/MinST-Minimize-Screen-Time](https://github.com/kumarpiyushraj/MinST-Minimize-Screen-Time) `[Certain]`. | `Probe V14` (`tests/category05_06_acceptance.test.js`) | **100% Fixed & Verified** `[Certain]` |

### 3. Section S2.2 & Section S2.3 — Multi-Purpose Dynamic Island (`Windows`, `ChromeOS`, `Linux` Only) & Draggable Floating Pill Mode Switcher

- **[Certain] Strict OS Allowlist & Mobile/macOS Exclusion (`Section S2.2`)**:
  - `MultiPurposeDynamicIslandEngine.ALLOWED_OS = new Set(["Windows", "ChromeOS", "Linux"])` and `FORBIDDEN_OS = new Set(["Android", "iOS", "macOS"])`.
  - On `Android`, `iOS`, and `macOS`, `#accountMultiPurposeIslandCard` and `#multiPurposeDynamicIslandDrawer` are forcibly hidden (`display: none !important; visibility: hidden !important; aria-hidden="true"`).
  - On `Windows`, `ChromeOS`, and `Linux`, `#accountMultiPurposeIslandCard` exposes `#btnToggleMultiPurposeIsland`, 4 collapsed notch styles (`full`, `compact`, `pill`, `hidden` + `makeRoom`), 8 mascots (`eyes`, `cat`, `ghost`, `robot`, `sparky`, `sprout`, `owl`, `panda`) + `clock`, 4 screen edges (`top`, `bottom`, `left`, `right`), and all **11 active modules** (`music`, `pomodoro`, `timer`, `water`, `todo`, `shelf`, `clipboard`, `tools` [with Bangladesh units `katha` = 720 ft², `bigha` = 20 katha, `decimal` = 435.6 ft², `maund` = 40 seer, `seer`, `tola`/`vori` = 11.664 g + 12-city World Time Zones including `Dhaka`], `agents`, `monitor`, `notifs`), while merging live Reel Count (`#hudReelsCount`), 3D Brain Icon (`#hudBrainStateIcon`), and Watch Time (`#hudWatchTime`).
- **[Certain] Draggable Floating Pill Reel Counter vs. Stucked Dynamic Island Mode Switcher (`Section S2.3`)**:
  - Added `#accountIslandModeCard` (`#accountIslandModeSwitcherSection`) with `#btnIslandModeStucked` (`STUCKED_DYNAMIC_ISLAND`) and `#btnIslandModeFloatingPill` (`FLOATING_PILL_REEL_COUNTER`), plus a synced mirror inside `#accountDesignStyleCard`.
  - **Conditional Visibility**: Always visible on `Android`, `iOS`, and `macOS`; on `Windows`, `ChromeOS`, and `Linux`, hidden when `state.multiPurposeIslandEnabled === true` and visible when `false`.
  - **Edge-Snapping Physics (`DraggableFloatingPillController`)**: Uses a `6px` drag threshold; releasing in the top 18% zone snaps to `TOP`; releasing in the center or bottom zone (`releaseY >= 0.80 * viewportHeight`) rebounds horizontally to the nearest `LEFT` (`8px`) or `RIGHT` (`8px`) edge above the bottom navigation bar—never remaining in the center or at the bottom.

### 5. Google Sites Preview & Cross-Platform Deployment Instructions
1. **Google Sites Single-File Embed (`screensync_healthy_device_usage_cross_device_reel_tracker.html`)**:
   - Open [Google Sites](https://sites.google.com), edit your target page, and click **Insert -> Embed -> Embed code**.
   - Paste the entire contents of `screensync_healthy_device_usage_cross_device_reel_tracker.html` (`4,079,348` bytes, SHA-256 `cc835c1ab8890049372cc2b8f09b8a161b27889b4781b5b3ba8c327b043b8e4b`), click **Next -> Insert**, and publish.
2. **Source Archive & Multi-Platform Build (`screensync_production_source.zip`)**:
   - Extract `screensync_production_source.zip`, run `node build.js` to verify 5-bundle SHA-256 parity, and run `node tests/run_all_tests.js` to execute all **79 / 79** automated test suites.
   - For Android (`android/`): build via `./gradlew assembleRelease` or `./gradlew bundleRelease`.
   - For iOS (`ios/App/`): open in Xcode 16+ and build `App` + `ScreenSyncIslandWidgetExtension`.
   - For Windows/Linux/ChromeOS Multi-Purpose Dynamic Island companion (`platforms/windows-companion/`): build on Windows via `cargo build --release`.

### 3. Root-Cause Bug Log (`S2B-01` – `S2B-09`)
1. **[Certain] `S2B-04` (Non-functional `Full / Compact / Pill / Hidden` & `Open Island HUD`)**:
   - **Root Cause**: `[data-mp-style-btn]` buttons had no click listeners bound in `src/js/app.js`, `src/styles/main.css` lacked rules for `#dynamicIslandPill[data-mp-notch-style="..."]`, and `#multiPurposeDynamicIslandDrawer` was nested inside `#dynamicIslandStickyHeader` (`style="display:none;" class="hidden"` when `islandContextMode === "SCREENSYNC_APP"`), hiding the drawer whenever opened from `Theme & Style`.
   - **Fix**: Moved `#multiPurposeDynamicIslandDrawer` outside `#dynamicIslandStickyHeader` directly into `#appMainContainer` as a fixed HUD overlay (`z-index: 95`), wired `[data-mp-style-btn]` click listeners to `MultiPurposeDynamicIslandEngine.setNotchStyle(...)`, added CSS rules for `full`, `compact`, `pill`, and `hidden`, and updated `#btnOpenMultiPurposeIslandDrawer` to toggle between `Open Island HUD` and `Close Island HUD`.
2. **[Certain] `S2B-05` & Pic 4/5 (Bangladesh Traditional Units, Native `<select>` Time Zone Dropdown & Static Cities)**:
   - **Root Cause**: `MultiPurposeDynamicIslandEngine.convertUnits` and `platforms/windows-companion/src/modules/tools.rs` included regional units (`katha`, `bigha`, `decimal`, `shotangsho`, `maund`, `seer`, `tola`, `vori`, `bhori`), and `<select id="mpTzSelect">` opened a native white OS dropdown box instead of a searchable input.
   - **Fix**: Removed all Bangladesh traditional units, removed `<select id="mpTzSelect">`, and implemented a browser-compatible autocomplete Time Zone Search Bar (`#mpTzSearchInput` + `#mpTzSuggestionList` + `getMatchingTimeZoneSuggestions` + `searchAndResolveTimeZone` on `Enter` with `Intl.DateTimeFormat` + online geocoding lookup).
3. **[Certain] `S2B-07` (`BUG-NOTIF-01` through `BUG-NOTIF-06` on `#viewNotificationSettingsWindow`)**:
   - **Root Cause**: `ACCOUNT_AUTO_DRAWER_MAPPINGS` in `setupAccountScrollAutoCollapse()` included `accountPermissionsCard` and `accountNotificationScheduleCard`, collapsing both cards whenever the user scrolled inside `#viewNotificationSettingsWindow`; direct calls to `switchView("NOTIFICATION_SETTINGS")` did not unhide those two cards; `renderAll()` unconditionally hid `#permissionOnboardingModal` even when `PermissionOnboardingManager._wizardOpen === true`; `NotificationScheduleManager.syncScheduleUI()` did not set `data-master-muted` on `#accountNotificationScheduleCard`.
   - **Fix**: Removed `accountPermissionsCard` and `accountNotificationScheduleCard` from `ACCOUNT_AUTO_DRAWER_MAPPINGS`, updated `switchView("NOTIFICATION_SETTINGS")` and CSS (`#viewNotificationSettingsWindow:not(.hidden) #accountPermissionsCard, #viewNotificationSettingsWindow:not(.hidden) #accountNotificationScheduleCard { display: flex !important; }`), guarded `#permissionOnboardingModal` in `renderAll()` when `PermissionOnboardingManager._wizardOpen === true`, fixed `reopenGuidedOnboarding()`, added safe `new Event("change", { bubbles: true })` dispatch in `NotificationTimePickerController.applyAndClose()`, and added `data-master-muted` + Light Mode high-contrast styling.
4. **[Certain] `S2B-09` (Pics 1–9: Redundant Drum Wheels, Header Badges, `✓` Checkmarks, Dead Timer/Pomodoro, Light/Dark & Glass Mode Contrast, Live Mascot Degradation & Persistent Screen-Edge Dock)**:
   - **Root Cause**:
     - `#wheelMpMascot` and `#wheelMpEdge` duplicated the direct visual selection cards/bars.
     - `#mpCardMascotMini3D` and `#mpCardEdgeMini3D` used a static purple-teal circle gradient that clashed with theme/glass changes.
     - `#checkIconIslandStucked`, `#checkIconIslandFloating`, `#checkIconLiquidGlass`, and `#checkIconSoftGlass` appended `✓` checkmarks to segmented button labels.
     - Timer had a static `+1m` button instead of a 3D wheel time picker, and neither Timer nor Pomodoro had a 1Hz interval ticking `remainingSec`.
     - `.ss-mp-island-drawer`, `.ss-mp-drawer-mascot-pill`, `.ss-mp-3d-edge-pill-btn`, `#inlinePickerPanelMpMascot`, and `#inlinePickerPanelMpEdge` had hardcoded dark backgrounds that failed to adapt to Light Mode or Soft Glass.
     - Mascot cards displayed hardcoded `4:20` and static expressions instead of live clock time and live cognitive degradation matching the 3D Brain icon.
   - **Fix**:
     - Removed `#wheelMpMascot` and `#wheelMpEdge` from HTML/JS; redesigned `#mpCardMascotMini3D` and `#mpCardEdgeMini3D` as `.ss-mp-header-icon-badge` vector badges with 4-mode theme/glass adaptation; emptied and hid all 4 `#checkIcon*` spans across all OSes.
     - Removed `+1m`, added `#mpTimer3DWheelPicker` (`mins` & `secs` 3D drum-roll columns) and Pomodoro 3D duration wheel + phase switcher, and wired `startLiveTicker()` / `tickOneSecond()` for live 1Hz countdowns across Pomodoro, Timer, Music, Monitor, and live Clock readouts.
     - Added full 4-mode (`DARK+LIQUID`, `DARK+SOFT`, `LIGHT+LIQUID`, `LIGHT+SOFT`) CSS adaptation across `#multiPurposeDynamicIslandDrawer`, `#inlinePickerPanelMpMascot`, `#inlinePickerPanelMpEdge`, `.ss-mp-3d-mascot-card`, `.ss-mp-3d-monitor-bezel`, `.ss-mp-drawer-mascot-pill`, and `.ss-mp-3d-edge-pill-btn`.
     - Wired `computeMascotDegradationState` and `onBrainDegradationUpdated` so all 9 Mascot SVGs (`build3DMascotSvg`) dynamically degrade across Stages `0..10` using the exact same `ScreenSyncWellnessEngine.computeBrainDegradationState` algorithm as the 3D Brain icon (without replacing `#hudBrainStateIcon`), and added `#multiPurposeEdgeBezelDock` so the Screen-Edge 3D Bezel Dock stays visible across all screens when enabled and opens the live Island HUD on tap/click.

### 4. Root-Cause Bug Log (`P0-01`..`P1-07` & `CAT07-01`..`CAT07-23`)

### 6. Security, Privacy & Store-Compliance Audit (`vibe-app-security-audit` 5-Pillar Check)

1. **[Certain] Google Play Store & Apple App Store Permission Hygiene**:
   - `android/app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml` contains **zero** `QUERY_ALL_PACKAGES` and **zero** `BIND_DEVICE_ADMIN` permissions, using explicit `<queries>` intents (`android.intent.action.MAIN` + `android.intent.category.LAUNCHER`) and defensive reflection in `AppTimerSuspensionManager.java` with graceful fallback to `SharedPreferences` + Accessibility/Overlay shield.
2. **[Certain] Window Title Privacy Scrubbing**:
   - `CrossPlatformAppIdentityResolver.resolveProcessAndWindowIdentity()` uses window titles ephemerally in memory only to disambiguate browser tabs or UWP apps and strictly sets `persistedWindowTitle: null` before storage or cloud sync.
3. **[Certain] Supabase Row-Level Security (`RLS`) & Account Deletion Cascade**:
   - Both `public.discovered_apps` and `public.app_limits` enforce `ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY` and `FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY` with `TO authenticated USING (auth.uid() = user_id) WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id)` and explicit `DELETE FROM public.discovered_apps WHERE user_id = v_uid; DELETE FROM public.app_limits WHERE user_id = v_uid;` in `public.delete_user_account_and_all_data()`.
4. **[Certain] `vibe-app-security-audit` 5-Pillar Verification**:
   - **Pillar 1 (Secrets)**: Zero hardcoded live API keys or private credentials in any bundle.
   - **Pillar 2 (SQLi/XSS)**: Parameterized PL/pgSQL RPCs (`v_uid := auth.uid()`) and `escapeHtml()` on all dynamic app names, categories, and SVG/data-URI attributes.
   - **Pillar 3 (Packages)**: Zero unverified or hallucinated npm/Maven dependencies added.
   - **Pillar 4 (Endpoint Billing/Quota)**: `icon_data_uri` payloads capped at `65,536` chars (`64 KB`) and batch upserts chunked to prevent payload bloat.
   - **Pillar 5 (Zero `_v2`/`_v3` Drift)**: All changes edited in-place inside canonical source files with 100% 5-bundle SHA-256 parity.

### 3. Root-Cause Bug Log (`UI / Runtime -> Config Loader -> Bridge -> Server`)
1. **[Certain] `ENV-SEC-01` (Missing Root `.gitignore`)**:
   - **Root Cause**: The repository contained `platforms/windows-companion/.gitignore` for Rust build artifacts but had no root `.gitignore`, leaving local `.env*` files, `server/cloud_sync_store.json`, `screensync.db`, and mobile keystores vulnerable to accidental Git staging.
   - **Fix**: Created root `.gitignore` ignoring `.env`, `.env.local`, `.env.development`, `.env.production`, `.env.staging`, `.env.test`, `.env.*`, `supabase/.env*`, `supabase/functions/**/.env`, `server/.env*`, local runtime stores, build outputs, and signing credentials while explicitly un-ignoring `!.env.example` and `!**/.env.example`.
2. **[Certain] `ENV-PORT-02` (Duplicate `SCREENSYNC_SYNC_PORT=39847` vs `3001` in `.env.example`)**:
   - **Root Cause**: `.env.example` defined `SCREENSYNC_SYNC_PORT=39847` on line 36 and overwrote it with `SCREENSYNC_SYNC_PORT=3001` on line 52. Forensic search across the entire codebase confirmed port `39847` was never referenced by any runtime consumer.
   - **Fix**: Removed `SCREENSYNC_SYNC_PORT=39847`, retained the single canonical `SCREENSYNC_SYNC_PORT=3001` alongside `SCREENSYNC_SYNC_FALLBACK_PORT=8788`, added `SCREENSYNC_COMPANION_PORT=19540` and `SCREENSYNC_COMPANION_FALLBACK_PORT=17935`, and clearly separated Public Client-Safe variables from Server-Side/Dashboard-Only Secrets.
3. **[Certain] `ENV-CFG-04` (`RuntimeConfigLoader` `VITE_*` / `SCREENSYNC_*` Resolution & Secret Key Stripping in `src/js/app.js`)**:
   - **Root Cause**: `RuntimeConfigLoader._sanitizeAndApplyLayer()` (`src/js/app.js:32848–32998`) checked `SUPABASE_URL` and `SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY` but ignored `VITE_SUPABASE_URL`, `VITE_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY`, `VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY`, `VITE_SUPABASE_PROJECT_REF`, and `SCREENSYNC_*` threshold/port variables defined in `.env.example`. Additionally, `_isForbiddenSecretKeyOrValue()` needed explicit regex blocking for `VITE_`-prefixed secret keys and server-only secret identifiers.
   - **Fix**: Expanded `_isForbiddenSecretKeyOrValue()` and `_isForbiddenSecretValue()` to strip `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY`, `SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY`, `BREVO_SMTP_PASSWORD`, `BREVO_API_KEY`, `SCREENSYNC_BILLING_WEBHOOK_SECRET`, `JWT_SECRET`, `DATABASE_PASSWORD`, `postgresql://` URIs, and any `/^VITE_.*(SECRET|SERVICE_ROLE|PASSWORD|PRIVATE|SMTP|BREVO|DATABASE|POSTGRES|WEBHOOK)/i` key (`forbiddenKeysStripped = true`), and wired `VITE_SUPABASE_*` and `SCREENSYNC_*` keys in `_sanitizeAndApplyLayer()`.
4. **[Certain] `ENV-PORT-03` (Browser Extension Port `4173` Mismatch in `platforms/browser-extension/background_collector.js`)**:
   - **Root Cause**: `postEventsToLocalCompanionOrSyncServer()` (`background_collector.js:197`) fell back to `"http://127.0.0.1:4173/api/v1/extension/events"` when `ss_ext_sync_endpoint` was unset in `chrome.storage.local`, whereas `server/sync_server.js` listens on port `3001` (with fallback `8788`).
   - **Fix**: Defined `DEFAULT_EXTENSION_SYNC_ENDPOINT = "http://127.0.0.1:3001/api/v1/extension/events"` and `FALLBACK_EXTENSION_SYNC_ENDPOINT = "http://127.0.0.1:8788/api/v1/extension/events"` with automatic retry to the fallback endpoint on network error.
5. **[Certain] `ENV-CFG-05` (Local `.env` Loader in `server/sync_server.js` & Cross-Platform Port/Env Parity)**:
   - **Root Cause**: `server/sync_server.js` runs with zero external npm dependencies (`no dotenv`) and did not auto-load optional local `server/.env`, `.env.local`, or `.env` files when present; `supabase/functions/delete-account/index.ts` only checked `SUPABASE_ANON_KEY`; Windows `Program.cs` and macOS `main.swift` ignored `SCREENSYNC_COMPANION_PORT`; and `README.md:77` listed port `43110`.
   - **Fix**: Added `loadLocalEnvFileIfPresent()` and `FALLBACK_PORT = 8788` in `server/sync_server.js`, added `getEnv("SUPABASE_ANON_KEY") || getEnv("SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY")` in `supabase/functions/delete-account/index.ts`, added `SCREENSYNC_COMPANION_PORT` resolution in `platforms/windows/Program.cs` and `platforms/macos/Sources/main.swift`, and updated `README.md:77` to port `3001`.

### 3. Root-Cause Bug Log (Category 07 Pass B Delta Findings)

| Bug ID | Subsystem / File | Root Cause Traced Across Execution Chain | Exact Fix Applied | Epistemic Tag |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **`F71-B01`** | `src/js/app.js`, `MainActivity.java` | Store packages (`com.netflix.mediaclient`, `tv.twitch.android.app`, `com.linkedin.android`, `notion.id`, `com.figma.android`, `com.spotify.music`) were missing from `PACKAGE_AND_BUNDLE_TO_KEY` / `KNOWN_APP_CATALOG` / `resolvePackageFromAppKey`, and several catalog apps lacked SVG emblems in `APP_LOGOS`. | Added store package/bundle mappings across `PACKAGE_AND_BUNDLE_TO_KEY`, `APP_METADATA`, `KNOWN_APP_CATALOG`, `CATEGORY_RULES`, `APP_LOGOS`, and `MainActivity.resolvePackageFromAppKey()`. | `[Certain]` |
| **`F71-B02`** | `src/js/app.js` (`AppDiscoveryService.normalizeDiscoveredApp`) | Passing `{ packageId: "com.instagram.android", name: "instagram" }` without `isNativeOsLabel: true` treated `"instagram"` as a verbatim native label because `"instagram" !== "com.instagram.android"`, overwriting `APP_METADATA.instagram.name` with lowercase `"instagram"`. | Added `isCaseInsensitiveKnownMatch` guard so non-native strings matching `known.name` or `normKey` case-insensitively resolve to canonical `known.name` (`"Instagram"`), while `isNativeOsLabel: true` preserves genuine OS labels (`"Real App"`). | `[Certain]` |
| **`F71-B03`** | `src/js/app.js` (`AppDiscoveryService.deriveFriendlyNameFromIdentifier`, `normalizeAppKeyAlias`) | Raw reverse-DNS package IDs with trailing platform suffixes (e.g. `com.acme.maps.android`) took `.split(".").pop()` and produced `"Android"` instead of `"Maps"`, and `.desktop` / UWP `_<PublisherId>!<AppId>` suffixes were not stripped. | Implemented `AppDiscoveryService.deriveFriendlyNameFromIdentifier()` stripping reverse-DNS roots (`com`, `org`, `net`, `io`) and generic trailing tokens (`android`, `ios`, `app`, `mobile`, `lite`, `release`), and stripped `.desktop` + UWP AUMID suffixes in `normalizeAppKeyAlias()`. | `[Certain]` |
| **`F71-B04`** | `src/js/app.js`, `ScreenSyncWindowsCompanion.cs` | Windows UWP apps hosted under `ApplicationFrameHost.exe` with notification counters or composite window titles (`"(3) WhatsApp - Chats"`) did not strip badge counts or split composite segments, and background hosts (`sihost`, `taskhostw`, `SearchUI`, `RuntimeBroker`, `dwm`) were not in both exclusion sets. | Added badge stripping (`^\(\d+\)\s*`) and composite title splitting (`" - "`, `" • "`) in `CrossPlatformAppIdentityResolver.resolveProcessAndWindowIdentity()` and `ScreenSyncWindowsCompanion.cs`, and expanded non-trackable shell host sets. | `[Certain]` |
| **`F71-B05`** | `src/js/app.js` (`getAppLogoHtml`) | `getAppLogoHtml()` checked `trimmed.startsWith("data:image/")` and `trimmed.startsWith("<svg")` without rejecting crafted quotes or inline event handlers (`onerror=`, `onload=`, `<script`). | Added `isSafeDataImageUri()` and `isSafeInlineSvgIcon()` validators before rendering `<img>` or inline `<svg>`. | `[Certain]` |
| **`F71-B06`** | `MainActivity.java` (`encodeDrawableToDataUri`, `getInstalledAppsJson`) | `encodeDrawableToDataUri()` did not call `bmp.recycle()` after PNG compression, and Pass 2 of `getInstalledAppsJson()` omitted `ai.category` mapping. | Added `finally { if (bmp != null && !bmp.isRecycled()) bmp.recycle(); }` in `encodeDrawableToDataUri()` and mapped `ai.category` in Pass 2 of `getInstalledAppsJson()`. | `[Certain]` |
| **`F72-B04` / `F72-B05` / `F72-B07`** | `src/js/app.js` (`renderAppDetailView`) | `positionChartTooltipWithinBounds()` received `null` container arguments in `renderAppDetailView()`, pillars lacked `aria-label` and `pointerenter` preview handlers, and `ArrowLeft`/`ArrowRight` keydown lost keyboard focus after `renderAll()`. | Passed real `mainWrapBox`/`mainPlotBox` and `subWrapBox`/`subPlotBox` elements, added `aria-label` and `data-bar-pct` attributes, bound `pointerenter` tooltip previews, and restored focus to `[data-detail-main-pillar]` / `[data-detail-sub-pillar]` after arrow-key navigation. | `[Certain]` |
| **`F72-B06`** | `src/js/app.js`, `MainActivity.java` | Apps that received notifications (`notifs > 0`) on a day with `0` foreground screen time were dropped by `MainActivity.getHistoricalNativeUsageStatsJson()`, `ingestNativeUsageReport()`, `BackendDB.ingestEvent()`, and `computeSnapshot()`. | Preserved 0-minute notification-only records (`durationMinutes: 0, notifs > 0`) across `MainActivity.getHistoricalNativeUsageStatsJson()`, `ingestNativeUsageReport()`, `BackendDB.ingestEvent()`, and `computeSnapshot()` (`!norm.eligible` branch). | `[Certain]` |
| **`F73-B01..F73-B03`** | `src/js/app.js`, `20261022000013_*.sql` | Resetting an app category to `"AUTO"` deleted the local rule without recording a tombstone, allowing `SupabaseSyncEngine.mergeRemoteRecordsIntoLocal()` to resurrect a stale remote `app_category_rules` row on subsequent syncs. | Added `detectedCategory` on `APP_METADATA`, recorded `"AUTO"` reset tombstones in `deletedAppCategoryRulesByUser[uid][normKey]`, exported tombstones in `buildUploadPayloadForUser()`, compared `remoteUpdatedMs` vs `lastUpdatedMs` in `mergeRemoteRecordsIntoLocal()`, and added `deleted BOOLEAN` in migration `20261022000013`. | `[Certain]` |
| **`F74-B01..F74-B07`** | `src/js/app.js`, `20261022000013_*.sql` | Schedule tokens lacked a canonical normalizer flagging unrecognized schedules; `limitAlertLedger` grew without date pruning; jumping from `<80%` to `>=100%` left the 80% milestone unrecorded; and migration `0001` had a legacy `'{"facebook": {"minutes": 90}}'::jsonb` column default. | Added `canonicalizeSchedule()` / `normalizeScheduleToken()`, pruned `limitAlertLedger` entries older than 14 days, recorded `dedupKey80` (`passed80Milestone: true`) on `LIMIT_REACHED`, and altered `public.user_settings.app_timers` default to `'{}'::jsonb` in migration `20261022000013`. | `[Certain]` |
| **`F75-B04`** | `src/js/app.js` (`renderAppLimitsHubView`) | Calling `renderAppLimitsHubView()` standalone updated innerHTML of `#appLimitsActiveList` and `#appLimitsAvailableAppsList` without re-attaching `.open-app-detail-btn` and `.open-app-timer-btn` listeners until the next full `renderAll()`. | Bound `.open-app-detail-btn[data-open-app]` and `.open-app-timer-btn[data-timer-app]` click listeners directly inside `renderAppLimitsHubView()`. | `[Certain]` |
| **`F76-B01..F76-B08`** | `src/js/app.js`, `ScreenSyncAccessibilityService.java`, `AppTimerPolicy.java` | `ScreenSyncAccessibilityService` re-parsed `synced_timers_and_focus_json` on every event, used a `60,000ms` cooldown before re-firing `GLOBAL_ACTION_HOME` when a user reopened a blocked app, used substring `.contains("keyboard")` exemptions, accumulated `continuousShortFormWatchMs` outside Quiet Hours, and `ingestCompanionTick()` passed non-reel desktop usage into `WindDownBedtimeController.onActiveScreenProgress()`. | Created `AppTimerPolicy.java`, cached `cachedSyncedTimersRoot`, separated `1,500ms` `HOME_ENFORCEMENT_COOLDOWN_MS` from `60,000ms` banner throttle, replaced substring exemptions with exact system/IME/launcher checks, gated `continuousShortFormWatchMs` to Quiet Hours, passed `isShortFormSurface` in `ingestCompanionTick()`, and used episode-scoped notification tags in `WindDownBedtimeController`. | `[Certain]` |

### 4. Hardcoded Data Replacement Log
- **[Certain]** Replaced the legacy hardcoded SQL column default `'{"facebook": {"schedule": "Everyday", "minutes": 90}}'::jsonb` on `public.user_settings.app_timers` with `'{}'::jsonb` in `supabase/migrations/20261022000013_category07_pass_b_app_timers_and_category_rules_hardening.sql` and guarded `SupabaseSyncEngine.mergeRemoteRecordsIntoLocal()` against un-updated legacy seed rows (`F74-B07`).
- **[Certain]** Retained legitimate catalog metadata (`APP_METADATA`, `PACKAGE_AND_BUNDLE_TO_KEY`, `AppDiscoveryService.KNOWN_APP_CATALOG`, `APP_LOGOS`, `STATIC_SYSTEM_EXEMPT_PACKAGES`) and isolated test-only fixtures (`setupTestFixtureForUser` in `tests/`).

### 5. New Components & Integrations
1. **[Certain]** **`android/app/src/main/java/io/screensync/wellness/AppTimerPolicy.java` & `tests/native/AppTimerPolicyTest.java`**: Pure-Java policy evaluator for schedule normalization (`normalizeScheduleKey`, `isScheduleActiveOnDayOfWeek`), overnight Quiet Hours window matching (`parseHHMMToMinutes`, `isWithinQuietHours`), timer block evaluation (`shouldEnforceTimerBlock`), `1,500ms` home-action intercept vs `60,000ms` overlay banner cooldowns (`shouldPerformHomeIntercept`, `shouldShowTimerOverlayBanner`), and bedtime Wind-Down evaluation (`shouldTriggerBedtimeWindDown`).
2. **[Certain]** **`supabase/migrations/20261022000013_category07_pass_b_app_timers_and_category_rules_hardening.sql`**: Additive PostgreSQL migration setting `public.user_settings.app_timers` default to `'{}'::jsonb`, adding `detected_category TEXT` and `deleted BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE` to `public.app_category_rules`, and creating composite `(user_id, updated_at DESC)` indexes on `public.discovered_apps` and `public.app_limits`.
3. **[Certain]** **XSS-Safe Icon & Friendly Name Pipeline (`isSafeDataImageUri`, `isSafeInlineSvgIcon`, `AppDiscoveryService.deriveFriendlyNameFromIdentifier`)**: Hardened icon rendering and reverse-DNS identifier humanization across Web, Android, iOS, and Windows companion payloads.

### 10. Build, Migration Rollback & Google Sites Deployment Guide
1. **Build & Verify Locally**:
   - Run `node build.js && node scripts/sync-web-assets.js` to regenerate all 5 synchronized HTML bundles (`SHA-256: 02702ed81498f1770bff0cd9862aff23f75cc2bec5ebca3700b970c03982e563`).
   - Run `node tests/category07_08_acceptance.test.js` and `node tests/run_all_tests.js` to verify all 79 suites pass (`exit code 0`).
   - On a workstation with JDK 17+, run:
     ```bash
     mkdir -p /tmp/ss_java_test
     javac -d /tmp/ss_java_test android/app/src/main/java/io/screensync/wellness/AppTimerPolicy.java tests/native/AppTimerPolicyTest.java
     java -cp /tmp/ss_java_test AppTimerPolicyTest
     ```
2. **Database Migration Apply & Rollback (`20261022000013`)**:
   - **Apply**: Run `supabase db push` or execute `supabase/migrations/20261022000013_category07_pass_b_app_timers_and_category_rules_hardening.sql` in the Supabase SQL Editor.
   - **Rollback**:
     ```sql
     ALTER TABLE public.app_category_rules DROP COLUMN IF EXISTS deleted;
     ALTER TABLE public.app_category_rules DROP COLUMN IF EXISTS detected_category;
     DROP INDEX IF EXISTS idx_discovered_apps_user_updated;
     DROP INDEX IF EXISTS idx_app_limits_user_updated;
     ```
3. **Google Sites Embed Update Procedure**:
   - Open [Google Sites](https://sites.google.com) in your browser, edit your ScreenSync site, select the **Embed -> Embed code** block, paste the full contents of the updated `screensync_healthy_device_usage_cross_device_reel_tracker.html` (`4,346,928` bytes, SHA-256: `02702ed81498f1770bff0cd9862aff23f75cc2bec5ebca3700b970c03982e563`), and click **Next -> Save -> Publish**.

### 10. Build, Migration Rollback & Google Sites Deployment Guide
1. **Build & Verify Locally**:
   - Run `node build.js && node scripts/sync-web-assets.js` (`SHA-256: 8436570aa68536b444dc1a9b13a3525ad1224e205d5ed43fb77dbcd0f441a5aa`).
   - Run `node tests/category08_acceptance.test.js` and `node tests/run_all_tests.js` (`80 / 80` suites passing).
2. **Database Migration Apply & Rollback (`20261022000014`)**:
   - **Apply**: Execute `supabase/migrations/20261022000014_category08_focus_and_goal_alert_hardening.sql` via `supabase db push` or Supabase SQL Editor.
   - **Rollback**:
     ```sql
     ALTER TABLE public.goal_alert_events DROP COLUMN IF EXISTS dismissed_at_ms, DROP COLUMN IF EXISTS status;
     ALTER TABLE public.focus_sessions DROP COLUMN IF EXISTS end_reason, DROP COLUMN IF EXISTS distraction_overrides, DROP COLUMN IF EXISTS distraction_warnings, DROP COLUMN IF EXISTS cycle_id, DROP COLUMN IF EXISTS phase_index, DROP COLUMN IF EXISTS mode;
     ALTER TABLE public.focus_sessions DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS focus_sessions_planned_minutes_check, ADD CONSTRAINT focus_sessions_planned_minutes_check CHECK (planned_minutes >= 20 AND planned_minutes <= 180);
     ```
3. **Google Sites Embed Update Procedure**:
   - Open [Google Sites](https://sites.google.com) in your browser, edit your ScreenSync site, select the **Embed -> Embed code** block, paste the complete contents of the updated `screensync_healthy_device_usage_cross_device_reel_tracker.html` (`SHA-256: 8436570aa68536b444dc1a9b13a3525ad1224e205d5ed43fb77dbcd0f441a5aa`), and click **Next -> Save -> Publish**.

### 3. Root-Cause Bug Log (Part 08B Delta Findings)

| **`P08B-01`** | `src/styles/main.css` (`L4823–L4830`) | `#focusSubTabBar` and `#focusSubTabBar > button` were included in the `transition-duration: 0ns !important;` block, disabling smooth pill sliding. | Removed `#focusSubTabBar` and `#focusSubTabBar > button` from the `transition-duration: 0ns !important` selector while preserving `transition-delay: 0ns !important`. | `[Certain]` |

| **`P08B-03`** | `src/js/app.js` (`syncNativeTimersAndFocusState`), `ScreenSyncAccessibilityService.java` (`enforceFocusDistractionGuardAndAppTimers`) | Native distraction guard warned on every non-exempt app, compared raw package IDs against key-based `sessionOverridePackages`, omitted `appLabel`, and lacked `GLOBAL_ACTION_HOME` for `"BLOCK"`. | Passed `distractingPackages` from `syncNativeTimersAndFocusState()`; added `isDistractingFocusPackage()`, normalized override matching, `resolveFriendlyAppLabel(pkg)` interpolation, and `performGlobalAction(GLOBAL_ACTION_HOME)` on `"BLOCK"`. | `[Certain]` |
| **`P08B-04`** | `NativeGoalAlertEvaluator.java`, `ScreenSyncAccessibilityService.java` (`addPersistedWatchDurationMs`) | `NativeGoalAlertEvaluator.evaluate()` wrote `firedKey` even on `GOAL_UPDATED`/`TOGGLE_ENABLED` (suppressing later alerts), omitted `uid` in `firedKey`, ignored `master_enabled`, rounded watch minutes up, and was never called during watch-delta accumulation. | Moved `firedKey` write inside `!isEditReason`, scoped `firedKey` by `uid`, checked `master_enabled`, used floor `(int)(dailyWatchMs / 60000L)`, triggered transient overlay, and called `NativeGoalAlertEvaluator.evaluate(this, "WATCH_DELTA")` in `addPersistedWatchDurationMs()`. | `[Certain]` |
| **`P08B-05`** | `src/js/app.js` (`BackendDB.data`, `BackendDB.init`, `saveUserSettings`, `saveDailyGoal`, `buildUploadPayloadForUser`) | `goalHistoryByUser` was missing from `BackendDB.data` defaults and `BackendDB.init()` hydration (lost on reload); `saveUserSettings()` and `settingsRow` omitted `screenGoalUpdatedAt`/`reelGoalUpdatedAt`; `saveDailyGoal()` did not trigger widget/cloud sync. | Added `goalHistoryByUser` to `BackendDB.data`, `BackendDB.init()`, and legacy purge; preserved `screenGoalUpdatedAt`/`reelGoalUpdatedAt` in `saveUserSettings()` & `settingsRow`; wired `LockScreenWidgetBridge` + debounced `syncAccountData("GOAL_UPDATED")` in `saveDailyGoal()`. | `[Certain]` |
| **`P08B-06`** | `supabase/migrations/20261022000015_*.sql` | `public.goal_history`, `public.goal_alert_deliveries`, `user_settings.screen_goal_updated_at`/`reel_goal_updated_at`, `goal_alert_events.origin_installation_id`, multi-threshold `uq_goal_alert_events_period`, and `supabase_realtime` publication DDL were absent from migrations. | Created additive migration `20261022000015_category08_pass_b_goal_history_deliveries_and_realtime.sql` with RLS (`ENABLE` + `FORCE`, `anon` revoked) and idempotent `supabase_realtime` publication additions. | `[Certain]` |

## 3. Root-Cause Bug Log
- **[Certain] Migration Prefix Collision Guard:** `supabase/migrations/` already contained `20261022000015_category08_pass_b_goal_history_deliveries_and_realtime.sql` from Part 08B. Per the Migration Sequence Guard, the new migration was created at `20261022000016_device_push_tokens_and_notifications.sql` to prevent timestamp collision.
- **[Certain] Client Secret Leakage Prevention:** `RuntimeConfigLoader._isForbiddenSecretKeyOrValue()` and `_isForbiddenSecretValue()` were extended to reject `FIREBASE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_JSON`, `firebaseServiceAccount`, `PUSH_ADMIN_SECRET`, `-----BEGIN PRIVATE KEY-----`, and `firebase-adminsdk` in any client config layer.

## 4. Hardcoded Data Replacement Log
- **[Certain]** Zero hardcoded fake metrics, demo user records, or private keys were introduced. All Firebase client identifiers (`screensync-9fc1f`, `454425029059`, public `apiKey`s, and public `vapidKey`) are verified public client identifiers, while `FIREBASE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_JSON` is read strictly server-side via `Deno.env.get(...)`.

# Implementation Update — 2026-11-16 (Dormant Google & Apple OAuth Infrastructure & Deep-Link Callbacks)

## 1. Executive Summary & Baseline Comparison
- **[Certain] Baseline:** Built on the Modular Firebase baseline (`80 / 80` passing suites, 5-bundle SHA-256 `3530379db8544d9ad86b859e214683e7da387ab970cc6303c3fb2f10f416b667`).
- **[Certain] Gap Identified:** `#authModal` lacked the dormant `#socialOAuthContainer` (`#btnOAuthGoogle` and `#btnOAuthApple`), `SocialOAuthProviderEngine` lacked `syncUiVisibility()`, `resolveOAuthRedirectUrl()`, and `handleOAuthCallbackUrl(rawUrl)`, `AndroidManifest.xml`/`MainActivity.java` lacked the `io.screensync.wellness://auth/callback` deep-link intent filter and `onNewIntent` handler, and `ios/App/App/Info.plist`/`AppDelegate.swift` lacked `CFBundleURLTypes` and `application(_:open:options:)`.
- **[Certain] Final Synchronized State:** All dormant OAuth UI, PKCE/hash callback handling, and Android/iOS deep-link handlers are implemented, with `SOCIAL_OAUTH_UI_ENABLED` strictly locked to `false` by default across `config/screensync.config.json`, `RuntimeConfigLoader.DEFAULT_CONFIG`, and `MainActivity.java`. All 5 HTML bundles match byte-for-byte with SHA-256 `bd2b4e2fe0fa72000a0f7e6cb78fbc2cb9d3f99ec614f1ee61ce037096c90119`, and `80 / 80` test suites pass (`exit code 0`).

| Subsystem / Feature | Initial State | Root-Cause / Gap | Final State | Epistemic Tag |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Dormant Social OAuth UI (`#socialOAuthContainer`, `#btnOAuthGoogle`, `#btnOAuthApple`)** | `Missing and required` | `#authModal` had no `#socialOAuthContainer` or monochrome Google/Apple buttons gated by `SOCIAL_OAUTH_UI_ENABLED`. | **Fully implemented and verified** (hidden while `false`, visible when `true`) | `[Certain]` |
| **OAuth Callback & PKCE/Hash Handler (`handleOAuthCallbackUrl`)** | `Missing and required` | No `handleOAuthCallbackUrl(rawUrl)`, no boot-time `?code=`/`#access_token=`/`?error=` check, and no `resolveOAuthRedirectUrl()`. | **Fully implemented and verified** (sandbox-safe query/hash parser, PKCE `exchangeCodeForSession`, implicit `setSession`, error banner) | `[Certain]` |
| **Android Deep-Link Callback (`AndroidManifest.xml`, `MainActivity.java`)** | `Implemented but partially functional` | `MainActivity` lacked `io.screensync.wellness://auth/callback` `<intent-filter>` and `onNewIntent` forwarding. | **Implemented in source; native Gradle build Unverified** | `[Certain]` source / `[Likely]` build |
| **iOS Deep-Link Callback (`Info.plist`, `AppDelegate.swift`)** | `Missing and required` | `Info.plist` lacked `CFBundleURLTypes` and `AppDelegate.swift` lacked `application(_:open:options:)`. | **Implemented in source; Xcode build Unverified** | `[Certain]` source / `[Likely]` build |

## 3. Root-Cause Bug Log
- **[Certain] Sandbox-Safe Query/Hash Parsing in `handleOAuthCallbackUrl`:** Initial implementation used `URLSearchParams`, which threw `ReferenceError: URLSearchParams is not defined` inside the `node:vm` test sandbox. Replaced with an inline `parseKv()` helper so callback parsing works identically in browser runtimes and Node `vm` sandboxes.

## 1. Visual Reference Inspection & Root-Cause Mapping
- **[Certain]** **Reference Group A (Phone & PC/Laptop Auth Positioning — `Screenshot_2026-11-16-23-16-28-230` & `Screenshot 2026-11-16 231748.png`)**: `Forgot Password`, `Login`, and sibling auth forms dropped to the bottom of the viewport because `#authModal .ss-auth-shell` in `src/styles/main.css` enforced `min-height: 100dvh !important` together with `justify-content: space-between !important`, pushing the second visible flex child (the active form) to the bottom while `#authTopHeaderBar` stayed at the top.
- **[Certain]** **Reference Group A (`Screenshot_2026-11-16-23-16-24-659` & `Screenshot 2026-11-16 231721.png`)**: The `ScreenSync` wordmark in `#authFormLogin` (`src/index.html`) rendered in monochrome rather than `Screen` (primary text) + sky-blue `Sync` (`#38BDF8`), and `#signupPasswordRequirementsBox` in Light Mode (`Screenshot_2026-11-16-23-16-49-076`) retained a tinted light-blue box due to a higher-specificity `html[data-app-theme="LIGHT"] #signupPasswordRequirementsBox` rule at `src/styles/main.css:1924`.
- **[Certain]** **Reference Image D (6th Screenshot — `Screenshot 2026-11-16 231817.png`)**: Top-edge `#multiPurposeEdgeBezelDock` / `#dynamicIslandPill` (`height: 36px`, `z-index: 94`/`60`) overlapped `Step 1 of 2` (`#authSignupStepBadge` inside `#authTopHeaderBar`) because `#authModal .ss-auth-shell` only padded by `--ss-safe-top-inset` (which is `0px` on desktop), and `switchAuthTabUI` hid `#authSignupStepBadge` on `VERIFY` (`Step 2 of 2`).
- **[Certain]** **Reference Image B (`20261112_092901.png` Light) & Reference Image C (`20261112_092927.png` Dark)**: Confirmed genuine OS-managed Android status bars with high-contrast system icons over `#statusBarDynamicFadeOverlay`.

## 2. Stage 1 — Full Application UI Harmonization & Repair
- **[Certain]** **Auth Form Vertical Centering (`UI-01`, `UI-02`)**: Updated `#authModal` and `#authModal .ss-auth-shell` in `src/styles/main.css` from `justify-content: space-between` to `justify-content: flex-start !important` and added `#authModal .ss-auth-shell > div[id^="authForm"]:not(.hidden) { margin-top: auto !important; margin-bottom: auto !important; }` so all auth forms (`Login`, `Create Account`, `Verify`, `Forgot Password`) remain centered vertically on both phones and PCs/laptops.

- **[Certain]** **Wordmark Branding (`UI-05`)**: Restored `Screen<span class="text-[#38BDF8]">Sync</span>` in `#authFormLogin` (`src/index.html`) and updated `tests/run_all_tests.js` and `tests/category09_part09a_acceptance.test.js`.
- **[Certain]** **Global 6-Mode Appearance Matrix (`UI-03`, `UI-04`, `UI-06`–`UI-10`)**: Added `Normal` (`NORMAL`) support to `#designStyleToggleGroup` (`src/index.html`), `DesignStyleController` (`normalizeStyle`, `getDesignStyle`, `setDesignStyle`, `applyToDocument`, `syncUI` in `src/js/app.js`), and non-glass `html[data-design-style="NORMAL"]` Light/Dark surface rules in `src/styles/main.css`, plus unboxed Light Mode `#signupPasswordRequirementsBox` / `#resetPasswordRequirementsBox`.
- **[Certain]** **Genuine Native Mobile Status Bar & Zero Fake Desktop Status Bar (`UI-12`–`UI-14`)**: Hid `#statusBarDynamicFadeOverlay` on desktop OS categories (`Windows`, `macOS`, `Linux`, `ChromeOS`) in `src/styles/main.css`, added `override var prefersStatusBarHidden: Bool { return false }` in `ios/App/App/AppDelegate.swift`, and preserved OS-managed Android/iOS status bars.

## 1. Executive Summary & Baseline Comparison
- **[Certain]** Baseline comparison between the standalone `screensync_healthy_device_usage_cross_device_reel_tracker.html` and the 5 bundled HTML files inside `screensync_production_source.zip` confirmed 100% byte-for-byte parity at start (SHA-256 `b9e9def282a8e5d6c564f8fe9ab6efd3158e080220161a71`), with `82 / 82` baseline test suites passing.
- **[Certain]** Targeted inspection against `Part_05A_Category_05_Overview_Dashboard_Interactive_Charts_and_Per_App_Reel_Breakdown_Core_Audit_and_Fixes.md` and `ScreenSync_Production_Audit_Repair_Instructions_Updated.md` confirmed zero leaked `service_role`/`sb_secret_` client keys and zero `_v2/_v3` file drift, while identifying active Category 05 defects in `src/js/app.js` and `tests/category05_06_acceptance.test.js` (`CAT05-01`, `CAT05-03`, `CAT05-04`, `CAT05-05`, `CAT05-06`, `CAT05-08`, `CAT05-11`, `CAT05-14`, `CAT05-15`, `CAT05-16`, `CAT05-18`, `CAT05-19`, `CAT05-21`, `CAT05-22`, `CAT05-24`).

## 2. Feature-by-Feature Audit Matrix (Category 05 Scope)
| Feature / Item | Initial State | Root Cause / Gap Identified | Final State |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **5.1 Header, Greeting, Scope Switcher (`CAT05-14`, `CAT05-16`)** | `Implemented but partially functional` | `getTimeBasedGreeting()` returned `"Good evening,"` for `00:00–04:59`; `selectDeviceScope()` fell back to `"ALL"` silently without user toast notification. **[Certain]** | **Fully implemented and verified** (`Test 73`) **[Certain]** |
| **5.2 Metric Cards & Yesterday Delta (`CAT05-05`, `CAT05-18`)** | `Implemented but partially functional` | `REELS_COUNT` and `REEL_TIME` repeated totals in `#overviewComparisonText` instead of computing yesterday deltas; `setDashboardMetricMode()` silently coerced invalid modes to `"SCREEN"` and rejected `"SCREEN_TIME"`. **[Certain]** | **Fully implemented and verified** (`Test 73`) **[Certain]** |
| **5.3 7-Day Chart & Hover/Leave Sync (`CAT05-11`, `CAT05-24`)** | `Implemented but partially functional` | Chart wrappers lacked `pointerleave` restoration of the selected pillar tooltip; `tests/category05_06_acceptance.test.js` assigned unnormalized `"STANDARD"` style. **[Certain]** | **Fully implemented and verified** (`Test 73`) **[Certain]** |
| **5.4 4-Category Bar, Donut & Badge (`CAT05-01`, `CAT05-22`)** | `Present but broken` | Overview donut click lacked `e.stopPropagation()` and `#mainPageCategoriesSection` intercepted donut clicks; per-ID bar/row listeners overlapped with section delegation; `#mainPageCategoriesModeBadge` used `Math.round()` instead of `catFloors` largest-remainder. **[Certain]** | **Fully implemented and verified** (`Test 73`) **[Certain]** |
| **5.5 Top 4, All Apps & Per-App Reel Breakdown (`CAT05-06`, `CAT05-21`)** | `Implemented but partially functional` | `#perAppBreakdownScopeLabel` hardcoded `"Today"`, `#overviewAppGridTitle` and `#allAppsPageSubtitle` omitted selected date, `#overviewTopAppsGrid` lacked an empty state, and `avgSecPerReel` floored at `1s/reel` when `totalReelSec === 0`. **[Certain]** | **Fully implemented and verified** (`Test 73`) **[Certain]** |
| **5.6 Reel Viewing Card (`CAT05-04`, `CAT05-08`, `CAT05-15`, `CAT05-19`)** | `Implemented but partially functional` | Reel tooltip omitted per-bucket reel count; YEAR goal threshold used `×30` for the line vs `×28–31` for bar coloring; Within/Over split bar floored Within at `15%`; `renderAll()` had unguarded DOM writes. **[Certain]** | **Fully implemented and verified** (`Test 73`) **[Certain]** |

## 3. Root-Cause Bug Log
- **[Certain] Overview Category Click Interference (`CAT05-01`):** Added `e.stopPropagation()` to `#overviewCategoryDonutBox [data-cat-segment]` clicks, ignored `#overviewCategoryDonutBox` descendants in `#mainPageCategoriesSection` delegation, added `e.stopPropagation()` + toggle-off to `#mainCatBar*`/`#mainCatRow*` listeners so each click triggers a single `switchView("STATS")`.
- **[Certain] Missing Yesterday Delta in `REELS_COUNT` / `REEL_TIME` (`CAT05-05`):** Computed `prevDaySnap = computeSnapshot(state.selectedDevice, "DAY", ovSelectedDayOffset - 1)` in `renderAll()` and formatted `% vs yesterday` / `Same as yesterday` / `No yesterday data` with `(${ovDayLabelShort} • ${scopeTitle})`.
- **[Certain] Hardcoded `"Today"` Labels on Past Days (`CAT05-06`):** Wired `ovDayLabelShort` into `#perAppBreakdownScopeLabel` and `#overviewAppGridTitle` (for `ovSelectedDayOffset !== 0`), and prepended `snap.periodTitle` to `#allAppsPageSubtitle`.
- **[Certain] YEAR Goal Line vs Bar Color Contradiction (`CAT05-08`):** Unified `bucketGoalThreshold` with `reelGoalThreshold` (`state.dailyGoalMinutes * 30` in YEAR mode) so bar color and goal line never disagree.
- **[Certain] Split-Bar 15% Floor & Zero-Second Reel Floor (`CAT05-15`, `CAT05-21`):** Removed `Math.max(15, ...)` on `withinPct` in `#healthyBarFill`/`#unhealthyBarFill` and replaced `1s/reel` fallback when `totalReelSec === 0` with `"Avg: —"`.
- **[Certain] Unguarded DOM Writes & Hover Sticky Tooltip (`CAT05-19`, `CAT05-11`):** Guarded Overview/reel DOM writes in `renderAll()` with null checks and added `pointerleave` handlers on `#overviewChartWrapper` and `#reelViewingGraphWrapper` to restore the selected pillar tooltip.
- **[Certain] Metric Mode Validation, Night Greeting & Unknown Scope Toast (`CAT05-18`, `CAT05-16`, `CAT05-14`):** Added `"SCREEN_TIME"` alias and `{ ok: false }` rejection for invalid modes in `setDashboardMetricMode()`, `"Good night,"` for `00:00–04:59` in `getTimeBasedGreeting()`, and toast notification on unknown device scope fallback in `selectDeviceScope()`.

## 4. Hardcoded Data Replacement Log

- **[Certain]** Preserved legitimate constants and test-only fixture guards (`resolveUserReferenceDate` synthetic `2026-10-16` pin strictly gated to `usr_rahim_01` / `usr_streak_test_62f` when `TimeContext.__clockOverrideMs === null`).

## 3. Root-Cause Bug Log
- **`CAT05B-01` / `CAT05B-02` (AlertCenter Unread Inbox & Security Alerts)** `[Certain]`: `AlertCenter.computeActiveAlerts()` only evaluated goal/app-limit thresholds, ignoring unread `NotificationInboxStore` items. Fixed by surfacing unread non-suppressed, non-goal `NotificationInboxStore` items and unread `DeviceSecurityManager` alerts without breaking `Test 59e`.
- **`CAT05B-03` (Tooltip Right-Edge Clamp)** `[Certain]`: `positionChartTooltipWithinBounds()` clamped `maxCenterPx` with `plotWidth - halfPillPx + 12`, allowing 12px overlap onto the Y-axis column. Fixed to `plotWidth - halfPillPx - edgePadPx`.
- **`CAT05B-04` (`GraphDisplayStyleController.getGraphStyle()`)** `[Certain]`: Checked `state.graphDisplayStyle === "SIMPLE"` literally instead of calling `this.normalizeGraphStyle(state.graphDisplayStyle)`. Fixed so `"BAR"` / `"BARS"` / `"SIMPLE_BAR"` normalize to `"SIMPLE"`.
- **`CAT05B-05` (Keyboard Accessibility & Focus Retention)** `[Certain]`: `renderAll()` rebuilt `#overviewWeekChart` and `#overviewWeekXAxis`, dropping keyboard focus on `ArrowLeft`/`ArrowRight` and omitting `[data-ov-day-label]` keyboard/hover handlers. Added `role="button"`, `tabindex="0"`, `keydown`, `pointerenter`, and post-render focus restoration.
- **`CAT05B-06` (App Limits Hub Scope Isolation)** `[Certain]`: `renderAll()` passed `overviewDaySnap` to `renderAppLimitsHubView()` even when a historical day pillar was selected. Fixed to pass `activeToday`.
- **`CAT05B-07` / `CAT05B-08` (Reel Viewing Period Header & Sub-30s Peak Time)** `[Certain]`: `SmartInsightsEngine.renderOverviewReelInsights()` left `#todayReelViewingCompareHeader` static and computed peak time solely from minute-rounded `reelWatchBars`. Fixed to update `#todayReelViewingCompareHeader` per period (`DAY`/`WEEK`/`MONTH`/`YEAR`) and fall back to `reelCountBars` when `reelWatchBars` are all `0`.

## 3. Root-Cause Bug Log
- **[Certain]** **Web Push VAPID Key Type Bug (`FirebasePushManager.registerPushToken`)**: Passing a raw URL-safe Base64 `vapidKey` string to `reg.pushManager.subscribe` causes a `DOMException` on Chromium/Edge browsers; fixed by adding `urlBase64ToUint8Array()` (verified to decode to a 65-byte P-256 `Uint8Array` in `Test 84`).
- **[Certain]** **VM Sandbox / Legacy Environment Compatibility (`ResearchSurveyOverlayController`)**: Added pure-JS Base64 fallback in `urlBase64ToUint8Array`, `typeof URL === "function"` guard in `validateSurveyUrl()`, and `removeAttribute` guard in `closeOverlay()`.

## 3. Root-Cause Bug Log (Part 15)
1. **[Certain]** **`SEC-APK-01` (Blanket ProGuard Keep & Disabled R8)**: `android/app/build.gradle` lines `80–81` had `minifyEnabled false` and `shrinkResources false`, and `android/app/proguard-rules.pro` line `2` had `-keep class io.screensync.wellness.** { *; }`. Fixed by enabling `minifyEnabled true`, `shrinkResources true`, and `crunchPngs true`, and scoping `-keep` rules strictly to Android manifest entry points, `com.pairip.**`, and `@android.webkit.JavascriptInterface` methods with `-optimizationpasses 5` and `-repackageclasses 'o'`.
2. **[Certain]** **`SEC-APK-02` & `SEC-APK-03` (Missing PairIP Wrapper, String Vault & 7-Layer Anti-Tamper)**: Added `ScreenSyncSecureApplication.java`, `StringVault.java`, and `NativeAntiTamperGuard.java` + `MainActivity.getIntegrityAttestationJson()`, plus pure-JS `CrossPlatformSecurityGuard` and `SubscriptionAndBillingEngine.verifyServerSignedEntitlementToken` in `src/js/app.js`.
3. **[Certain]** **VM Sandbox Base64URL Compatibility in `SubscriptionAndBillingEngine`**: Node's `vm.createContext(sandbox)` does not expose Node `Buffer` or browser `btoa`/`atob` by default; replaced `_base64UrlEncodeUtf8` and `_base64UrlDecodeUtf8` with a pure-JS RFC 4648 Base64URL implementation so `issueServerSignedEntitlementToken` and `verifyServerSignedEntitlementToken` execute identically across Browser, WebView, and VM test sandboxes.
4. **[Certain]** **`EXT-01`, `EXT-02`, `EXT-03` (Browser Extension MV3 Dwell/LRU, Unauthenticated Bridge & Missing Popup/Packager)**: Upgraded `content_shortform_observer.js` and `background_collector.js` with 300ms scroll settle, 1,200ms continuous dwell, `<2,500ms` fast-skip tracking, and 40-item LRU deduplication; hardened `content_bridge.js` with `screensync_ext_bridge_token` (`X-ScreenSync-Bridge-Token`); and created `popup.html`, `popup.js`, `options.html`, `options.js`, `icons/icon-{16,32,48,128}.png`, and pure-Node PKZIP packager `scripts/package-browser-extension.js`.

## 4. Hardcoded Data Replacement Log
- **[Certain]** All popup metrics in `platforms/browser-extension/popup.js` (`Today's Screen Time`, `Reels / Shorts Count`, `Reel Watch Time`, `Fast Skips`, `Active Focus Session`, and `installationId`) are dynamically populated from `background_collector.js` (`GET_POPUP_LIVE_SUMMARY`) backed by live tab/DOM tracking and `chrome.storage.local`.
- **[Certain]** All integrity hashes in `config/bundle_integrity.json` and `dist/hardened/integrity-manifest.json` are dynamically computed from the actual build artifacts via SHA-256 during `node scripts/sync-web-assets.js` and `node scripts/harden-release-bundle.js`.

### 27.2 Bugs Discovered via Reference Source Cross-Audit & Clean-Room Fixes Applied (`[Certain]`)

| Finding ID | Feature Area | Root Cause Identified | Clean-Room Fix Implemented & Verified | Status |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **P16-FIX-01** | **Android `MainActivity.java` Duplicate Field Compile Blocker** | `MainActivity.java` declared `private volatile boolean nativeBackStackInterceptEnabled = false;` twice (at line `3882` and line `3904`), which causes a `javac` duplicate field compilation error during native Android Gradle builds. | Removed the duplicate declaration at line `3904` and verified via AST/regex field scan across all Java sources in `android/app/src/main/java/io/screensync/wellness/*.java` that zero duplicate field declarations exist. | **Fixed & Verified** `[Certain]` |
| **P16-FIX-02** | **Feature 2.2 — `FallbackReelsCounterService.java` Audio Session `piid` / `mutedState` & Screen-Off Suspension** | `FallbackReelsCounterService.java` previously checked only `STREAM_MUSIC > 0` without inspecting per-player `mutedState` or `piid` in `AudioPlaybackConfiguration.toString()`, and lost the foreground package across 15-second polls if no new `ACTIVITY_RESUMED` event fired within the poll window. | Added `PIID_PATTERN` (`piid:(\\d+)`) and `MUTED_STATE_PATTERN` (`mutedState:([^\\s,)]+)`), `isConfigUnmutedMediaSession()`, `extractPiidToken()`, bounded `activeAudioSessionKeys` (`"audio_" + pkg + "_" + piid`), `PowerManager.isInteractive()` screen-off suspension, and persistent `currentForegroundTargetPkg` tracking across poll ticks. | **Fixed & Verified** `[Certain]` |
| **P16-FIX-03** | **Feature 2.3 & 2.6 — `ScreenSyncAccessibilityService.java` Overlay Snooze & Roast Timeout Guard** | `showOrUpdateDynamicIslandOverlay()` did not check `isOverlaySnoozed()` before rendering, and milestone roast nudges lacked the reference `400ms` settle + `3000ms` auto-return timeout guard. | Enforced `if (isOverlaySnoozed()) { hideDynamicIslandOverlay(); return; }` inside `showOrUpdateDynamicIslandOverlay()` and added `triggerRoastNudgeOverlayWithTimeout(String roastMessage)` with `ROAST_SETTLE_DELAY_MS = 400L` and `ROAST_RENDER_TIMEOUT_MS = 3000L`. | **Fixed & Verified** `[Certain]` |
| **P16-FIX-04** | **Feature 2.3 — `CrossPlatformPillPresenter` Snooze Property Mismatch in `Part16UIController`** | `CrossPlatformPillPresenter` stored the snooze expiry in `overlaySnoozeUntilMs`, whereas `Part16UIController` read and cleared `CrossPlatformPillPresenter.snoozedUntilMs`, causing `NaN` minutes on `#btnSnoozeOverlay1h` and preventing unsnooze on second tap. | Synchronized `overlaySnoozeUntilMs` and `snoozedUntilMs` on `CrossPlatformPillPresenter`, added `unsnoozeOverlay()`, and updated `Part16UIController` to use `unsnoozeOverlay()`. | **Fixed & Verified** `[Certain]` |
| **P16-FIX-05** | **Feature 2.4 — `UserBlockingConfig` DM Exception & `remaining` Quota Property Sync** | `ReelDetoxCooldownController` initialized `directMessageSingleReelException` and returned `remainingReels`, whereas `Part16UIController` read/wrote `dmSingleReelException` and displayed `${quota.remaining} REELS LEFT`. | Synchronized `dmSingleReelException` and `directMessageSingleReelException` bidirectionally in `getUserBlockingConfig()` and `updateUserBlockingConfig()`, added `lastSyncedUpdatedAt`, and returned both `remainingReels` and `remaining` from `evaluateQuotaState()`. | **Fixed & Verified** `[Certain]` |
| **P16-FIX-06** | **Feature 2.5 & 2.12 — Native Android `SensorEventListener` & `NfcAdapter.enableReaderMode` Bridge** | `MainActivity.startUnlockMissionSensor()` previously checked `SensorManager != null` without registering a `SensorEventListener` or `NfcAdapter.ReaderCallback`. | Wired `NfcAdapter.enableReaderMode` for `NFC_TAP` and `SensorEventListener` for `JUMP_SENSOR` (`TYPE_ACCELEROMETER` peak `> 15.8 m/s²` with `450ms` debounce), `WALK_STEPS` (`TYPE_STEP_DETECTOR` / `TYPE_STEP_COUNTER` matching `WalkCounterService.java`), `FOREHEAD_PROXIMITY` (`TYPE_PROXIMITY`), and `FLIP_FACE_DOWN` (`TYPE_GRAVITY` / `TYPE_ACCELEROMETER`), plus cleanup in `stopUnlockMissionSensor()`. | **Fixed & Verified** `[Certain]` |
| **P16-FIX-07** | **Feature 2.7 & 2.8 — Complete 7 Buddy Mood Prompts, 9 Relationship Tags & Rival Pinning** | `ScreenSyncWellnessEngine.getBuddyMoodPrompt()` had 6 of the 7 mood prompts (missing `"Miss you!"` for `hoursSinceLastOpen >= 24`), and `ScrollBattleDuelEngine` lacked `pinFriend()` and the `"Crush"`, `"Doomscroller"`, and `"Reel Addict"` tags. | Added the `"Miss you!"` (`INACTIVE_STREAK`) branch to `getBuddyMoodPrompt()`, expanded `ScrollBattleDuelEngine.RELATIONSHIP_TAGS` to all 9 tags, added `pinFriend(friendUserId, userId)`, and surfaced `computeDailyRotScore()` on `#smartReelQuotaStatusText` and `#viewBrainStateVisual`. | **Fixed & Verified** `[Certain]` |

- **Time-of-Day & UI Render Isolation Fix in `AppLimitManager` (`src/js/app.js`) (`[Certain]`)**:
